var HANDS=[],INST=[],INST_T=[];
function parseHand(p){
  return p.split(' ').map(function(s){var d=s.indexOf('.'),c=+s.slice(0,d),sp=s.slice(d+1),g={c:c},m;
    if(sp==='F')g.k='F';
    else if(sp.slice(0,2)==='W?'){g.k='WV';g.v=sp.charAt(2);}
    else if(sp.slice(0,2)==='D?'){g.k='DV';g.v=sp.charAt(2);}
    else if(/^D[abc]$/.test(sp)){g.k='DS';g.s=sp.charAt(1);}
    else if(/^[NEWS]$/.test(sp)){g.k='T';g.t={N:27,E:28,W:29,S:30}[sp];}
    else if(/^[RGO]$/.test(sp)){g.k='T';g.t={R:31,G:32,O:33}[sp];}
    else if((m=/^(x(?:\+(\d))?|(\d))([abc])$/.exec(sp))){g.k='N';g.s=m[4];if(m[3])g.n=+m[3];else{g.x=1;g.o=m[2]?+m[2]:0;}}
    else throw new Error('bad token '+s);
    return g;});
}
function suitPerms(L){var out=[];(function rec(i,used,cur){if(i===L.length){var o={};for(var k in cur)o[k]=cur[k];out.push(o);return;}for(var s=0;s<3;s++)if(!(used&(1<<s))){cur[L[i]]=s;rec(i+1,used|(1<<s),cur);}})(0,0,{});return out;}
function prodVals(vars,vals){var out=[{}];vars.forEach(function(v){var n=[];out.forEach(function(o){vals.forEach(function(x){var c={};for(var k in o)c[k]=o[k];c[v]=x;n.push(c);});});out=n;});return out;}
function expandCard(){
  HANDS=[];INST=[];INST_T=[];for(var t=0;t<NT;t++)INST_T[t]=[];
  CARD.forEach(function(row,hi){
    var h={i:hi,cat:row[0],name:row[1],pts:row[2],conc:!!row[3],p:row[4],note:row[5]||'',gs:parseHand(row[4]),inst:[]};HANDS.push(h);
    var L=[],DV=[],WV=[],hasX=false,mo=0,tot=0;
    h.gs.forEach(function(g){tot+=g.c;if(g.s&&L.indexOf(g.s)<0)L.push(g.s);if(g.k==='DV'&&DV.indexOf(g.v)<0)DV.push(g.v);if(g.k==='WV'&&WV.indexOf(g.v)<0)WV.push(g.v);if(g.x){hasX=true;mo=Math.max(mo,g.o);}});
    if(tot!==14)throw new Error(h.name+' has '+tot+' tiles');
    L.sort();var seen={},xs=[];if(hasX){for(var x=1;x+mo<=9;x++)xs.push(x);}else xs.push(0);
    var P=suitPerms(L),D=prodVals(DV,[31,32,33]),W=prodVals(WV,[27,28,29,30]);
    P.forEach(function(ps){xs.forEach(function(x){D.forEach(function(dv){W.forEach(function(wv){
      var ts=[],cs=[],ok=true,used={};
      h.gs.forEach(function(g){var t;
        if(g.k==='F')t=34;else if(g.k==='T')t=g.t;else if(g.k==='WV')t=wv[g.v];else if(g.k==='DV')t=dv[g.v];else if(g.k==='DS')t=DRAGON_OF[ps[g.s]];
        else t=ps[g.s]*9+(g.x?x+g.o:g.n)-1;
        if(used[t])ok=false;used[t]=1;ts.push(t);cs.push(g.c);});
      if(!ok)return;
      var ord=ts.map(function(_,i){return i;}).sort(function(a,b){return ts[a]-ts[b];});
      var key=ord.map(function(i){return ts[i]+'x'+cs[i];}).join(',');if(seen[key])return;seen[key]=1;
      var gi=[];for(var t2=0;t2<NT;t2++)gi[t2]=-1;ts.forEach(function(t3,i){gi[t3]=i;});
      var I={n:INST.length,h:hi,ts:ts,cs:cs,gi:gi,conc:h.conc,pts:h.pts};INST.push(I);h.inst.push(I.n);
      ts.forEach(function(t4){INST_T[t4].push(I.n);});
    });});});});
  });
}
expandCard();

/* ---------- matching ---------- */
function expMatch(I,exps){var m=0;for(var k=0;k<exps.length;k++){var e=exps[k],gi=I.gi[e.t];if(gi<0||I.cs[gi]!==e.c||(m&(1<<gi)))return -1;m|=1<<gi;}return m;}
/* tiles still missing from rack+exposures to make instance I (jokers fill groups of 3+). av = naturals still out there (hard bots) */
function dist(I,cnt,exps,av){
  if(I.conc&&exps.length)return 99;var m=expMatch(I,exps);if(m<0)return 99;
  var pd=0,bd=0,ts=I.ts,cs=I.cs;
  for(var i=0;i<ts.length;i++){if(m&(1<<i))continue;var t=ts[i],c=cs[i],n=cnt[t];
    if(c<3){if(n<c){pd+=c-n;if(av&&av[t]<c)return 99;}}
    else if(n<c)bd+=c-n;}
  return pd+Math.max(0,bd-cnt[JK]);
}
function isWin(I,cnt,exps,rackN){
  if(I.conc&&exps.length)return false;var m=expMatch(I,exps);if(m<0)return false;
  var need=0,used=0;
  for(var i=0;i<I.ts.length;i++){if(m&(1<<i))continue;var t=I.ts[i],c=I.cs[i],n=cnt[t];
    if(n>c)return false;if(c<3&&n<c)return false;need+=c-n;used+=n;}
  return need===cnt[JK]&&used+cnt[JK]===rackN;
}
function findWin(rack,exps){
  var tiles=rack.length;exps.forEach(function(e){tiles+=e.c;});if(tiles!==14)return -1;
  var cnt=counts(rack),best=-1;
  /* only instances that use the first non-joker type of the hand */
  var t0=-1;for(var t=0;t<JK;t++)if(cnt[t]){t0=t;break;}
  var list=t0<0?INST.map(function(I){return I.n;}):INST_T[t0];
  for(var k=0;k<list.length;k++){var I=INST[list[k]];if(isWin(I,cnt,exps,rack.length)&&(best<0||I.pts>INST[best].pts))best=I.n;}
  return best;
}
/* best few instances for a rack (top-K by distance, small preference for higher value, open hands) */
function topInsts(cnt,exps,av,onlyH,K){
  var out=[];
  for(var i=0;i<INST.length;i++){var I=INST[i];if(onlyH>=0&&I.h!==onlyH)continue;var d=dist(I,cnt,exps,av);if(d>=40)continue;
    var s=d+(I.conc?0.7:0)-I.pts*0.012;
    if(out.length<K||s<out[out.length-1].s){var e={i:i,d:d,s:s},j=out.length;out.push(e);while(j>0&&out[j-1].s>s){out[j]=out[j-1];j--;}out[j]=e;if(out.length>K)out.pop();}}
  return out;
}
function visCounts(G){var v=[];for(var t=0;t<NT;t++)v[t]=0;G.dis.forEach(function(id){v[TT(id)]++;});G.seats.forEach(function(sd){sd.exp.forEach(function(e){e.ids.forEach(function(id){v[TT(id)]++;});});});return v;}
function availOf(G){var v=visCounts(G),a=[];for(var t=0;t<NT;t++)a[t]=TOT[t]-v[t];return a;}

/* ---------- game state ---------- */
var CH_STD=[{d:1,nm:'right'},{d:2,nm:'across'},{d:3,nm:'left'},{v:1},{d:3,nm:'left'},{d:2,nm:'across'},{d:1,nm:'right'},{d:2,c:1,nm:'across'}];
var CH_SKIP=[{d:1,nm:'right'},{d:2,nm:'across'},{d:3,nm:'left'},{d:2,c:1,nm:'across'}];
function nextSeat(G,s){for(var k=1;k<=4;k++){var o=(s+k)%4;if(!G.seats[o].dead)return o;}return -1;}
function pushEv(G,ev){G.evn=(G.evn||0)+1;ev.n=G.evn;G.ev=ev;G.evs=(G.evs||[]).concat([ev]).slice(-10);return ev;}
function dealHand(G,rng){
  var w=[];for(var i=0;i<152;i++)w.push(i);shuffle(w,rng);
  G.dealer=(G.first+G.hand)%4;G.hand++;
  G.seats.forEach(function(sd,i){sd.rack=w.splice(0,i===G.dealer?14:13);sd.exp=[];sd.dead=false;sd.tgt=-1;sd.calls=0;});
  G.wall=w;G.dis=[];G.last=null;G.turn=G.dealer;G.need='';G.drawn=-1;G.cl=null;G.elig=[];G.res=null;G.dl=0;G.cdl=0;
  G.phase='char';G.ch={i:0,st:G.copt==='skip'?CH_SKIP:CH_STD,picks:[null,null,null,null],votes:[null,null,null,null]};
  pushEv(G,{kind:'deal',seat:-1});
}
function startPlay(G){G.phase='play';G.ch=null;G.turn=G.dealer;G.need='discard';G.drawn=-1;pushEv(G,{kind:'play',seat:G.dealer});}
function chStage(G){return G.ch?G.ch.st[G.ch.i]:null;}
function advCh(G){G.ch.i++;G.ch.picks=[null,null,null,null];G.ch.votes=[null,null,null,null];if(G.ch.i>=G.ch.st.length)startPlay(G);}
function resolvePass(G){
  var st=chStage(G),P=G.ch.picks,k=4;
  if(st.c){var give=[0,0,0,0];[[0,2],[1,3]].forEach(function(pr){var n=Math.min(P[pr[0]].length,P[pr[1]].length);give[pr[0]]=n;give[pr[1]]=n;});
    for(var s=0;s<4;s++)P[s]=P[s].slice(0,give[s]);}
  var moved=[];
  for(var s2=0;s2<4;s2++){var sd=G.seats[s2];P[s2].forEach(function(id){var j=sd.rack.indexOf(id);if(j>=0)sd.rack.splice(j,1);});}
  for(var s3=0;s3<4;s3++){var to=(s3+st.d)%4;P[s3].forEach(function(id){G.seats[to].rack.push(id);});moved.push(P[s3].length);}
  var ev=pushEv(G,{kind:'passed',seat:-1,d:st.d,c:st.c?1:0,nm:st.nm,cnt:moved,got:P.map(function(_,s){return P[(s+4-st.d)%4].slice();})});
  advCh(G);return ev;
}
function canExpose(G,s,t,c){
  if(t===JK)return false;var sd=G.seats[s];if(sd.dead)return false;
  var cnt=counts(sd.rack);if(cnt[t]+cnt[JK]<c-1)return false;
  var exps=sd.exp.concat([{t:t,c:c}]),L=INST_T[t];
  for(var k=0;k<L.length;k++){var I=INST[L[k]];if(!I.conc&&I.cs[I.gi[t]]===c&&expMatch(I,exps)>=0)return true;}
  return false;
}
function canMjWith(G,s,id){var sd=G.seats[s];if(sd.dead)return false;return findWin(sd.rack.concat([id]),sd.exp)>=0;}
function callOpts(G,s){ /* what seat s may do with the tile just thrown */
  var o={mj:false,c:[]};if(!G.last||G.last.by===s||G.seats[s].dead)return o;var t=TT(G.last.id);if(t===JK)return o;
  o.mj=canMjWith(G,s,G.last.id);[3,4,5].forEach(function(c){if(canExpose(G,s,t,c))o.c.push(c);});return o;
}
function takeFor(rack,t,n){ /* naturals of type t first, then jokers */
  var out=[];for(var i=0;i<rack.length&&out.length<n;i++)if(TT(rack[i])===t)out.push(rack[i]);
  for(var j=0;j<rack.length&&out.length<n;j++)if(TT(rack[j])===JK)out.push(rack[j]);return out;
}
function rm(rack,ids){ids.forEach(function(id){var j=rack.indexOf(id);if(j>=0)rack.splice(j,1);});}
function nextDraw(G,from){
  var nx=nextSeat(G,from),live=G.seats.filter(function(sd){return !sd.dead;}).length;
  if(nx<0||!G.wall.length||live<2){endHand(G,-1,'wall',-1);return;}
  var id=G.wall.pop();G.seats[nx].rack.push(id);G.turn=nx;G.phase='play';G.need='discard';G.drawn=id;G.last=null;G.cl=null;G.elig=[];
  pushEv(G,{kind:'draw',seat:nx,id:id});
}
function checkDead(G){
  var v=null,jk=0;
  G.seats.forEach(function(sd,s){
    if(sd.dead||!sd.exp.length)return;
    if(!v){v=visCounts(G);jk=TOT[JK]-v[JK];}
    var t0=sd.exp[0].t,L=INST_T[t0],ok=false;
    for(var k=0;k<L.length&&!ok;k++){var I=INST[L[k]];if(I.conc)continue;var m=expMatch(I,sd.exp);if(m<0)continue;
      var need=0,bad=false;
      for(var i=0;i<I.ts.length;i++){if(m&(1<<i))continue;var t=I.ts[i],c=I.cs[i],a=TOT[t]-v[t];if(c<3){if(a<c){bad=true;break;}}else need+=Math.max(0,c-a);}
      if(!bad&&need<=jk)ok=true;}
    if(!ok){sd.dead=true;pushEv(G,{kind:'dead',seat:s});}
  });
}
function endHand(G,w,how,by){
  var res={hand:G.hand,w:w,how:how,by:by,pay:[0,0,0,0],h:-1,val:0,jl:false};
  if(w>=0){
    var sd=G.seats[w],ii=findWin(sd.rack,sd.exp),I=INST[ii],jl=true;
    sd.rack.forEach(function(id){if(TT(id)===JK)jl=false;});sd.exp.forEach(function(e){e.ids.forEach(function(id){if(TT(id)===JK)jl=false;});});
    var val=I.pts*(jl?2:1);res.h=I.h;res.inst=ii;res.val=val;res.jl=jl;
    for(var s=0;s<4;s++){if(s===w)continue;var p=how==='self'?2*val:(s===by?2*val:val);res.pay[s]=-p;res.pay[w]+=p;}
    for(var s2=0;s2<4;s2++)G.seats[s2].pts+=res.pay[s2];
  }
  res.tot=G.seats.map(function(x){return x.pts;});
  G.res=res;G.hist.push({hand:res.hand,w:w,how:how,h:res.h,val:res.val,pay:res.pay.slice()});
  G.phase=G.hand>=G.hands?'over':'round';G.need='';G.cl=null;G.elig=[];G.dl=0;G.cdl=0;
  if(G.phase==='over'){var bi=0;G.seats.forEach(function(x,i){if(x.pts>G.seats[bi].pts)bi=i;});G.win=bi;}
  pushEv(G,{kind:w>=0?'mj':'wall',seat:w,how:how,by:by});
}
function resolveCalls(G){
  var by=G.last.by,id=G.last.id,t=TT(id),pick=-1,kind='';
  for(var k=1;k<4&&pick<0;k++){var s=(by+k)%4,c=G.cl[s];if(c&&c.mj){pick=s;kind='mj';}}
  for(var k2=1;k2<4&&pick<0;k2++){var s2=(by+k2)%4,c2=G.cl[s2];if(c2&&c2.c){pick=s2;kind='exp';}}
  if(pick<0){nextDraw(G,by);return;}
  G.dis.pop();var sd=G.seats[pick];
  if(kind==='mj'){sd.rack.push(id);G.turn=pick;pushEv(G,{kind:'call',seat:pick,mj:1,id:id,by:by});endHand(G,pick,'discard',by);return;}
  var c=G.cl[pick].c,ids=takeFor(sd.rack,t,c-1);rm(sd.rack,ids);
  sd.exp.push({t:t,c:c,ids:[id].concat(ids)});sd.calls++;
  G.turn=pick;G.phase='play';G.need='discard';G.drawn=-1;G.last=null;G.cl=null;G.elig=[];
  pushEv(G,{kind:'call',seat:pick,c:c,t:t,id:id,by:by});
  checkDead(G);
}
/* apply one action for seat s; returns the event or null when it isn't allowed */
function apply(G,s,a){
  if(!G||!a)return null;
  if(G.phase==='char'){
    var st=chStage(G);if(!st||s<0||s>3)return null;
    if(a.t==='pass'&&!st.v){
      if(G.ch.picks[s])return null;var ids=a.ids;if(!Array.isArray(ids))return null;
      if(st.c?ids.length>3:ids.length!==3)return null;
      var rk=G.seats[s].rack,u={};for(var i=0;i<ids.length;i++){var x=ids[i];if(u[x]||rk.indexOf(x)<0||TT(x)===JK)return null;u[x]=1;}
      G.ch.picks[s]=ids.slice();var ev=pushEv(G,{kind:'picked',seat:s});
      if(G.ch.picks.every(function(p){return !!p;}))return resolvePass(G);
      return ev;
    }
    if(a.t==='vote'&&st.v){
      if(G.ch.votes[s]!==null)return null;G.ch.votes[s]=a.go?1:0;var ev2=pushEv(G,{kind:'vote',seat:s,go:a.go?1:0});
      if(G.ch.votes.every(function(v){return v!==null;})){
        var all=G.ch.votes.every(function(v){return v===1;});
        if(all)advCh(G);else{G.ch.i=G.ch.st.length-2;advCh(G);}
        return pushEv(G,{kind:'voted',seat:-1,go:all?1:0});
      }
      return ev2;
    }
    return null;
  }
  if(G.phase==='play'){
    if(s!==G.turn||G.need!=='discard')return null;var sd=G.seats[s];
    if(a.t==='discard'){
      var j=sd.rack.indexOf(a.id);if(j<0)return null;sd.rack.splice(j,1);G.dis.push(a.id);G.last={id:a.id,by:s};G.drawn=-1;
      var ev3=pushEv(G,{kind:'discard',seat:s,id:a.id});
      checkDead(G);
      var elig=[];if(TT(a.id)!==JK)for(var k=1;k<4;k++){var o=(s+k)%4,co=callOpts(G,o);if(co.mj||co.c.length)elig.push(o);}
      if(elig.length){G.phase='call';G.cl=[null,null,null,null];G.elig=elig;G.need='';}
      else nextDraw(G,s);
      return ev3;
    }
    if(a.t==='mj'){if(findWin(sd.rack,sd.exp)<0)return null;pushEv(G,{kind:'call',seat:s,mj:1,self:1});endHand(G,s,'self',-1);return G.ev;}
    if(a.t==='swap'){
      var p=G.seats[a.p];if(!p)return null;var e=p.exp[a.e];if(!e)return null;var hi=sd.rack.indexOf(a.id);if(hi<0||TT(a.id)!==e.t)return null;
      var jj=-1;e.ids.forEach(function(id,q){if(jj<0&&TT(id)===JK)jj=q;});if(jj<0)return null;
      var jid=e.ids[jj];e.ids[jj]=a.id;sd.rack.splice(hi,1);sd.rack.push(jid);
      return pushEv(G,{kind:'swap',seat:s,p:a.p,e:a.e,id:a.id,jid:jid});
    }
    return null;
  }
  if(G.phase==='call'){
    if(a.t==='close'){G.elig.forEach(function(o){if(!G.cl[o])G.cl[o]={no:1};});resolveCalls(G);return G.ev;}
    if(G.elig.indexOf(s)<0||G.cl[s])return null;
    if(a.t==='nocall')G.cl[s]={no:1};
    else if(a.t==='call'){var co2=callOpts(G,s);if(a.mj){if(!co2.mj)return null;G.cl[s]={mj:1};}else{if(co2.c.indexOf(+a.c)<0)return null;G.cl[s]={c:+a.c};}}
    else return null;
    var evc=pushEv(G,{kind:'claim',seat:s,go:G.cl[s].no?0:1});
    if(G.elig.every(function(o){return !!G.cl[o];}))resolveCalls(G);
    return evc;
  }
  return null;
}
/* who still has to act: [seats] */
function pending(G){
  if(!G)return [];
  if(G.phase==='char'){var st=chStage(G),o=[];for(var s=0;s<4;s++)if(st.v?G.ch.votes[s]===null:!G.ch.picks[s])o.push(s);return o;}
  if(G.phase==='play')return [G.turn];
  if(G.phase==='call')return G.elig.filter(function(s){return !G.cl[s];});
  return [];
}

/* ---------- computer players ---------- */
function dangerOf(G,s){ /* tiles other players with exposures probably want */
  var d=[];for(var t=0;t<NT;t++)d[t]=0;
  G.seats.forEach(function(o,os){if(os===s||o.dead||!o.exp.length)return;
    var L=INST_T[o.exp[0].t],hits=[];for(var k=0;k<L.length;k++){var I=INST[L[k]];if(I.conc)continue;var m=expMatch(I,o.exp);if(m>=0)hits.push([I,m]);}
    if(!hits.length)return;var w=(o.exp.length>=2?1.6:0.7)/hits.length;
    hits.forEach(function(h){for(var i=0;i<h[0].ts.length;i++)if(!(h[1]&(1<<i)))d[h[0].ts[i]]+=w;});});
  return d;
}
function botTop(G,s,skill,K){
  var sd=G.seats[s],cnt=counts(sd.rack),av=skill==='hard'?availOf(G):null,top=[];
  if(skill==='easy'&&sd.tgt>=0){top=topInsts(cnt,sd.exp,av,sd.tgt,K);if(!top.length||top[0].d>7)sd.tgt=-1;}
  if(!top.length||skill!=='easy')top=topInsts(cnt,sd.exp,av,-1,K);
  if(skill==='easy'&&sd.tgt<0&&top.length){sd.tgt=INST[top[Math.floor(Math.random()*Math.min(4,top.length))].i].h;top=topInsts(cnt,sd.exp,av,sd.tgt,K);}
  return {cnt:cnt,av:av,top:top};
}
function scoreRack(top,cnt,exps,av){var sc=0;for(var k=0;k<top.length;k++)sc+=dist(INST[top[k].i],cnt,exps,av)/(k+1);return sc;}
/* the tile to let go of: the one whose loss hurts the best hands least */
function worstTiles(G,s,skill,n,noJ){
  var b=botTop(G,s,skill,skill==='easy'?4:8),cnt=b.cnt,sd=G.seats[s],out=[],taken={};
  var dg=skill==='hard'&&G.phase!=='char'?dangerOf(G,s):null,late=G.wall?Math.max(0,1-G.wall.length/70):0;
  for(var r=0;r<n;r++){
    var best=-1,bs=1e9;
    for(var t=0;t<NT;t++){if(!cnt[t]||t===JK)continue;
      cnt[t]--;var sc=scoreRack(b.top,cnt,sd.exp,b.av);cnt[t]++;
      if(dg)sc+=dg[t]*late*late*1.2;
      if(skill==='easy')sc+=Math.random()*0.5;else sc+=Math.random()*0.05;
      if(sc<bs){bs=sc;best=t;}}
    if(best<0)break;cnt[best]--;
    for(var i=sd.rack.length-1;i>=0;i--){var id=sd.rack[i];if(TT(id)===best&&!taken[id]){taken[id]=1;out.push(id);break;}}
  }
  if(!out.length&&!noJ&&sd.rack.length)out.push(sd.rack[sd.rack.length-1]);
  return out;
}
function botSwap(G,s,skill){
  if(skill==='easy')return null;var sd=G.seats[s];
  for(var p=0;p<4;p++){var ex=G.seats[p].exp;for(var e=0;e<ex.length;e++){var X=ex[e];if(!X.ids.some(function(id){return TT(id)===JK;}))continue;
    var mine=sd.rack.filter(function(id){return TT(id)===X.t;});if(!mine.length)continue;
    if(skill==='normal'&&Math.random()<0.35)continue;
    var b=botTop(G,s,skill,6),cnt=b.cnt,cur=b.top.length?b.top[0].d:99;cnt[X.t]--;cnt[JK]++;
    var nt=topInsts(cnt,sd.exp,b.av,-1,1),nd=nt.length?nt[0].d:99;
    if(nd<=cur)return {t:'swap',id:mine[0],p:p,e:e};}}
  return null;
}
function botAct(G,s,skill){
  var sd=G.seats[s];skill=skill||'normal';
  if(G.phase==='char'){
    var st=chStage(G);
    if(st.v){var b=botTop(G,s,skill,4),d=b.top.length?b.top[0].d:9;return {t:'vote',go:skill==='easy'?Math.random()<0.8:d>=4||Math.random()<0.25};}
    if(st.c){var w=worstTiles(G,s,skill,3,true),b2=botTop(G,s,skill,6),keep=[];
      var base=scoreRack(b2.top,b2.cnt,sd.exp,b2.av);
      w.forEach(function(id){var c=b2.cnt;c[TT(id)]--;var nd=scoreRack(b2.top,c,sd.exp,b2.av);c[TT(id)]++;if(nd<=base+0.6||skill==='easy')keep.push(id);});
      return {t:'pass',ids:skill==='easy'?keep.slice(0,Math.floor(Math.random()*4)):keep.slice(0,3)};}
    return {t:'pass',ids:worstTiles(G,s,skill,3,true)};
  }
  if(G.phase==='play'){
    if(findWin(sd.rack,sd.exp)>=0)return {t:'mj'};
    var sw=botSwap(G,s,skill);if(sw)return sw;
    return {t:'discard',id:worstTiles(G,s,skill,1)[0]};
  }
  if(G.phase==='call'){
    var co=callOpts(G,s);if(co.mj)return {t:'call',mj:1};
    if(!co.c.length)return {t:'nocall'};
    if(skill==='easy'&&Math.random()<0.3)return {t:'nocall'};
    var t=TT(G.last.id),b3=botTop(G,s,skill,6),cur=b3.top.length?b3.top[0].d:99,best=null,bd=99;
    co.c.forEach(function(c){var ids=takeFor(sd.rack,t,c-1),cnt=b3.cnt.slice();ids.forEach(function(id){cnt[TT(id)]--;});
      var jk=ids.filter(function(id){return TT(id)===JK;}).length;
      var nt=topInsts(cnt,sd.exp.concat([{t:t,c:c}]),b3.av,skill==='easy'?sd.tgt:-1,1);if(!nt.length)return;var nd=nt[0].d+(skill==='hard'?jk*0.15:0);
      if(nd<bd){bd=nd;best=c;}});
    var lim=skill==='hard'?6:skill==='normal'?6:8;
    if(best&&bd<cur&&bd<=lim)return {t:'call',c:best};
    return {t:'nocall'};
  }
  return null;
}
function newGameState(o,rng){
  var G={v:1,gid:o.gid||'g'+Math.floor(rng()*1e9).toString(36),hands:o.hands||8,hand:0,first:Math.floor(rng()*4),copt:o.copt||'std',stake:o.stake||0,
    seats:o.seats.map(function(x){return {id:x.id,name:x.name,kind:x.kind,skill:x.skill||'normal',rack:[],exp:[],pts:0,dead:false,tgt:-1,calls:0};}),
    hist:[],phase:'setup',evn:0,ev:null,evs:[]};
  return G;
}
/*ENDENGINE*/


/* =====================================================================
   Tile faces: original art generated as SVG once at boot
   ===================================================================== */
var SERIF="font-family=\"Georgia,'Times New Roman',serif\"";
function faceSVG(t){
  var o='<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 60 82">';
  function idx(n,c){return '<text x="5.5" y="13" font-size="11" font-weight="700" font-family="Georgia,serif" fill="'+c+'">'+n+'</text>';}
  function lab(s,c){return '<text x="30" y="78" text-anchor="middle" font-size="7.5" font-weight="700" letter-spacing="1.2" font-family="Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif" fill="'+(c||'#7a5512')+'">'+s+'</text>';}
  if(t<9){
    var n=t+1,B='#1d4ed8',G='#15803d',Rr='#b91c1c';
    var P={1:[[31,44,15]],2:[[31,27,10],[31,62,10]],3:[[18,22,8.5],[31,44,8.5],[44,66,8.5]],4:[[20,28,8.5],[42,28,8.5],[20,60,8.5],[42,60,8.5]],
      5:[[19,24,8],[43,24,8],[31,44,8],[19,64,8],[43,64,8]],6:[[20,20,7.5],[42,20,7.5],[20,44,7.5],[42,44,7.5],[20,68,7.5],[42,68,7.5]],
      7:[[16,15,6.5],[31,22,6.5],[46,29,6.5],[20,50,6.5],[42,50,6.5],[20,69,6.5],[42,69,6.5]],8:[[20,15,6.5],[42,15,6.5],[20,33,6.5],[42,33,6.5],[20,51,6.5],[42,51,6.5],[20,69,6.5],[42,69,6.5]],
      9:[[17,20,6.3],[31,20,6.3],[45,20,6.3],[17,44,6.3],[31,44,6.3],[45,44,6.3],[17,68,6.3],[31,68,6.3],[45,68,6.3]]}[n];
    var C={1:[Rr],2:[G,B],3:[B,Rr,G],4:[B,G,G,B],5:[B,G,Rr,G,B],6:[G,G,Rr,Rr,Rr,Rr],7:[G,G,G,Rr,Rr,Rr,Rr],8:[B,B,B,B,B,B,B,B],9:[B,B,B,Rr,Rr,Rr,G,G,G]}[n];
    P.forEach(function(p,i){var c=C[i];o+='<circle cx="'+p[0]+'" cy="'+p[1]+'" r="'+p[2]+'" fill="'+c+'"/><circle cx="'+p[0]+'" cy="'+p[1]+'" r="'+(p[2]*0.64).toFixed(1)+'" fill="#fffaf0"/><circle cx="'+p[0]+'" cy="'+p[1]+'" r="'+(p[2]*0.36).toFixed(1)+'" fill="'+c+'"/>';
      if(n===1)o+='<circle cx="31" cy="44" r="21" fill="none" stroke="#15803d" stroke-width="2.5"/><circle cx="31" cy="44" r="18" fill="none" stroke="#1d4ed8" stroke-width="1.2"/>';});
    return o+idx(n,'#1d4ed8')+'</svg>';
  }
  if(t<18){
    var m=t-8;
    if(m===1){ /* one bam: a saguaro */
      o+='<path d="M14 75 H50" stroke="#b45309" stroke-width="2.6" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M26 74 V30 a6 6 0 0 1 12 0 V74 Z" fill="#15803d"/>'+
        '<path d="M26 54 H20 a5 5 0 0 1 -5 -5 V38 a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 7 0 V47 H26 Z" fill="#15803d"/><path d="M38 47 H43 a3 3 0 0 0 3 -3 V31 a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 7 0 V44 a9 9 0 0 1 -9 9 H38 Z" fill="#15803d"/>'+
        '<path d="M32 28 V72 M29 34 V70 M35 34 V70" stroke="#0b5a2a" stroke-width="1" opacity=".7"/><circle cx="32" cy="23" r="4" fill="#ec4899"/><circle cx="32" cy="23" r="1.6" fill="#f2d27e"/>';
      return o+idx(1,'#15803d')+'</svg>';
    }
    var S={2:[[31,28],[31,62]],3:[[31,28],[20,62],[42,62]],4:[[20,28],[42,28],[20,62],[42,62]],5:[[18,28],[44,28],[31,45],[18,62],[44,62]],
      6:[[18,28],[31,28],[44,28],[18,62],[31,62],[44,62]],7:[[31,19],[18,45],[31,45],[44,45],[18,69],[31,69],[44,69]],
      8:[[16,19],[27,19],[38,19],[49,19],[16,69],[27,69],[38,69],[49,69]],9:[[18,19],[31,19],[44,19],[18,45],[31,45],[44,45],[18,69],[31,69],[44,69]]}[m];
    var h=m>=7?17:24;
    S.forEach(function(p,i){var red=(m===5&&i===2)||(m===7&&i===0)||(m===9&&i>=3&&i<=5)||(m===8&&(i===1||i===6)),c=red?'#b91c1c':'#15803d',x=p[0],y=p[1];
      o+='<rect x="'+(x-3.6)+'" y="'+(y-h/2)+'" width="7.2" height="'+h+'" rx="3.6" fill="'+c+'"/><rect x="'+(x-1.1)+'" y="'+(y-h/2+2.5)+'" width="2.2" height="'+(h-5)+'" rx="1.1" fill="#fff" opacity=".38"/><rect x="'+(x-4.4)+'" y="'+(y-1)+'" width="8.8" height="2" rx="1" fill="#0b3b1d" opacity=".55"/>';});
    if(m===8)o+='<path d="M14 36 L48 54 M48 36 L14 54" stroke="#15803d" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M14 36 L48 54 M48 36 L14 54" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="1.3" opacity=".35"/>';
    return o+idx(m,'#15803d')+'</svg>';
  }
  if(t<27){
    var k=t-17;
    /* American-style crak: big numeral, a little western scroll, CRAK underneath */
    o+='<text x="31" y="51" text-anchor="middle" font-size="42" font-weight="700" '+SERIF+' fill="#b91c1c">'+k+'</text>'+
       '<path d="M17 59.5 C20 55.5 25 55.5 31 59.5 C37 63.5 42 63.5 45 59.5" fill="none" stroke="#b91c1c" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"/>'+
       '<circle cx="16.4" cy="59.8" r="1.6" fill="#b91c1c"/><circle cx="45.6" cy="59.2" r="1.6" fill="#b91c1c"/>'+
       '<text x="31" y="74" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" font-weight="700" letter-spacing="1.4" '+SERIF+' fill="#b91c1c">CRAK</text>';
    return o+idx(k,'#b91c1c')+'</svg>';
  }
  if(t<31){
    var L=['N','E','W','S'][t-27],W=['NORTH','EAST','WEST','SOUTH'][t-27];
    o+='<path d="M30 9 L33 15 L30 13 L27 15 Z" fill="#b45309"/><text x="30" y="58" text-anchor="middle" font-size="46" font-weight="700" font-family="Georgia,\'Times New Roman\',serif" fill="#0f2a4a">'+L+'</text>'+lab(W,'#0f2a4a');
    return o+'</svg>';
  }
  if(t===31||t===32){ /* dragon emblem: a coiled ring with flame tips around a big initial */
    var dc=t===31?'#b91c1c':'#15803d',ring='';
    for(var e=0;e<8;e++){var ea=e*Math.PI/4+Math.PI/8,ex=30+23*Math.cos(ea),ey=38+23*Math.sin(ea);ring+='<path d="M'+(30+19.5*Math.cos(ea-0.16)).toFixed(1)+' '+(38+19.5*Math.sin(ea-0.16)).toFixed(1)+' L'+ex.toFixed(1)+' '+ey.toFixed(1)+' L'+(30+19.5*Math.cos(ea+0.16)).toFixed(1)+' '+(38+19.5*Math.sin(ea+0.16)).toFixed(1)+' Z" fill="'+dc+'"/>';}
    return o+ring+'<circle cx="30" cy="38" r="19.5" fill="none" stroke="'+dc+'" stroke-width="2.6"/><circle cx="30" cy="38" r="16" fill="none" stroke="'+dc+'" stroke-width="0.9"/>'+
      '<text x="30" y="49.5" text-anchor="middle" font-size="31" font-weight="700" '+SERIF+' fill="'+dc+'">'+(t===31?'R':'G')+'</text>'+lab(t===31?'RED':'GREEN',dc)+'</svg>';
  }
  if(t===33)return o+'<rect x="13" y="11" width="34" height="54" rx="3" fill="none" stroke="#1d4ed8" stroke-width="3.6"/><rect x="19" y="17" width="22" height="42" rx="2" fill="none" stroke="#1d4ed8" stroke-width="1.4"/>'+lab('SOAP','#1d4ed8')+'</svg>';
  if(t===34){ /* desert bloom */
    o+='<path d="M30 72 C30 62 31 54 30 42" stroke="#15803d" stroke-width="2.6" fill="none"/><path d="M30 62 C22 61 17 55 16 50 C23 50 28 55 30 60Z" fill="#16a34a"/><path d="M30 56 C38 55 43 49 44 44 C37 45 32 50 30 54Z" fill="#16a34a"/>';
    for(var q=0;q<6;q++)o+='<ellipse cx="30" cy="20" rx="5.6" ry="11" fill="'+(q%2?'#f472b6':'#db2777')+'" transform="rotate('+(q*60)+' 30 31)"/>';
    o+='<circle cx="30" cy="31" r="5" fill="#f2d27e" stroke="#b45309" stroke-width="1.4"/><circle cx="28.5" cy="29.5" r="1.4" fill="#fff8dc"/>'+lab('FLOWER','#be185d');
    return o+'</svg>';
  }
  /* joker: a gold sheriff star */
  var pts6='',cx=30,cy=36,R1=22,R2=11.5,tips='';
  for(var i=0;i<12;i++){var a=-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/6,r=i%2?R2:R1,x=cx+r*Math.cos(a),y=cy+r*Math.sin(a);pts6+=x.toFixed(1)+','+y.toFixed(1)+' ';if(!(i%2))tips+='<circle cx="'+x.toFixed(1)+'" cy="'+y.toFixed(1)+'" r="3.3" fill="url(#jg)" stroke="#7a5512" stroke-width="1"/>';}
  o+='<defs><linearGradient id="jg" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fff1b8"/><stop offset=".5" stop-color="#f2c94c"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#b8862f"/></linearGradient></defs>'+
    '<polygon points="'+pts6+'" fill="url(#jg)" stroke="#7a5512" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linejoin="round"/>'+tips+
    '<circle cx="30" cy="36" r="7.5" fill="none" stroke="#7a5512" stroke-width="1.3"/><circle cx="30" cy="36" r="3" fill="#7a5512"/>'+
    '<text x="30" y="74" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="900" letter-spacing="1.4" font-family="Georgia,serif" fill="#8b1d1d">JOKER</text>';
  return o+'</svg>';
}
function svgUrl(s){return 'url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(s)+'")';}
function artUrl(u){return 'url("'+String(u).replace(/["\\\s]/g,encodeURIComponent)+'")';}
(function buildFaces(){
  var css='';
  for(var t=0;t<NT;t++){var u=t===JK&&ART_JOKER?artUrl(ART_JOKER):t===FL&&ART_FLOWER?artUrl(ART_FLOWER):ART_FACES[t]?artUrl(ART_FACES[t]):svgUrl(faceSVG(t));css+='.ty'+t+'{--f:'+u+'}';}
  var back='<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 60 82"><rect x="6" y="6" width="48" height="70" rx="6" fill="none" stroke="#f2d27e" stroke-opacity=".45" stroke-width="1.5"/><path d="M30 26 L33.5 36.5 L44.5 36.5 L35.5 43 L39 53.5 L30 47 L21 53.5 L24.5 43 L15.5 36.5 L26.5 36.5 Z" fill="#f2d27e" opacity=".8"/></svg>';
  css+='.tile.tb{--tback:'+(ART_TILE_BACK?artUrl(ART_TILE_BACK):svgUrl(back))+'}';
  if(ART_TABLE)css+='#stage{--tableart:'+artUrl(ART_TABLE)+'}';
  var st=document.createElement('style');st.textContent=css;document.head.appendChild(st);
  if(ART_TABLE)stage.classList.add('art');
})();
function tileH(id,cls){var t=TT(id);return '<span class="tile ty'+t+(t===JK?' jk':'')+(cls?' '+cls:'')+'" data-id="'+id+'" aria-label="'+tName(t)+'"></span>';}
function typeH(t,cls){return '<span class="tile ty'+t+(t===JK?' jk':'')+(cls?' '+cls:'')+'" aria-label="'+tName(t)+'"></span>';}

/* =====================================================================
   The card as text: suit letters are colors
   ===================================================================== */
function patHTML(h){
  return h.gs.map(function(g,gi){var ch,cl;
    if(g.k==='F'){ch='F';cl='f';}
    else if(g.k==='T'){ch={27:'N',28:'E',29:'W',30:'S',31:'R',32:'G',33:'\u25a1'}[g.t];cl='h';}
    else if(g.k==='WV'){ch='\u25c7';cl='g';}
    else if(g.k==='DV'){ch='D';cl='g';}
    else if(g.k==='DS'){ch='D';cl=g.s;}
    else {ch=String(g.x?1+g.o:g.n);cl=g.s;}
    return '<i class="'+cl+'" data-g="'+gi+'">'+new Array(g.c+1).join(ch)+'</i>';}).join('');
}
function nearHands(seat){
  if(!G||!G.seats||seat<0)return [];var sd=G.seats[seat],cnt=counts(sd.rack),out=[];
  HANDS.forEach(function(h){var bd=99;h.inst.forEach(function(i){var d=dist(INST[i],cnt,sd.exp,null);if(d<bd)bd=d;});if(bd<40)out.push({h:h.i,d:bd});});
  out.sort(function(a,b){return a.d-b.d||HANDS[b.h].pts-HANDS[a.h].pts;});return out;
}
function cardHTML(){
  var ms=mySeat(),near=ms>=0&&G&&G.seats&&G.phase!=='setup'?nearHands(ms):[],dm={},close={};near.forEach(function(x,i){dm[x.h]=x.d;if(i<3)close[x.h]=1;});
  var won=G&&G.res&&G.res.h>=0&&(G.phase==='round'||G.phase==='over')?G.res.h:-1;
  var h='<div class="mcard"><div class="ch"><h3>The Saddle Up Card</h3><span>'+HANDS.length+' hands</span></div>';
  if(near.length)h+='<div class="near">'+near.slice(0,3).map(function(x){return '<button type="button" data-trk="'+x.h+'">'+esc(HANDS[x.h].name)+'<i>'+(x.d?x.d+' away':'Ready!')+'</i></button>';}).join('')+'</div>';
  h+='<p class="lg"><span class="a">Green</span>, <span class="b">red</span> and <span class="c">blue</span> are three different suits (any suits). A colored D is the dragon that goes with that suit (Dots&ndash;Soap, Bams&ndash;Green, Craks&ndash;Red); a gold D is any dragon, &#9671; any wind, F a flower, &#9633; Soap. Runs shown from 1 may start on any number. X = you may call and expose, C = concealed. Tap a hand to track it on your rack.</p>';
  CATS.forEach(function(c){
    h+='<div class="cat"><b>'+c.n+'</b><small>'+c.d+'</small></div>';
    HANDS.forEach(function(hd){if(hd.cat!==c.k)return;var d=dm[hd.i];
      h+='<button type="button" class="hrow'+(close[hd.i]?' close':'')+(TRK===hd.i?' trk':'')+(won===hd.i?' won':'')+'" data-trk="'+hd.i+'"><span class="pat">'+patHTML(hd)+'</span><span class="xc'+(hd.conc?' c':'')+'">'+(hd.conc?'C':'X')+'</span><span class="pp">'+hd.pts+'</span>'+
        '<span class="hn"><b>'+esc(hd.name)+'</b>'+esc(hd.note)+(d!==undefined&&d<=5?'<em>'+(d?d+' away':'Ready')+'</em>':'')+'</span></button>';});
  });
  return h+'</div>';
}
var cardSig='';
function renderCardSide(force){
  if(!felt.classList.contains('wide')&&sheetKind!=='card'){cardSig='';return;}
  var ms=mySeat(),sig=(G&&G.seats&&ms>=0?G.gid+G.hand+':'+G.seats[ms].rack.slice().sort().join(',')+':'+G.seats[ms].exp.length+':'+G.phase:'none')+':'+TRK;
  if(sig===cardSig&&!force)return;cardSig=sig;
  var html=cardHTML();
  if(felt.classList.contains('wide')){var ci=$('cardIn'),st=ci.scrollTop;ci.innerHTML=html;ci.scrollTop=st;}
  if(sheetKind==='card'){var sc=$('ov').scrollTop;$('sheet').querySelector('.cardbox').innerHTML=html;$('ov').scrollTop=sc;}
  if(TIP_T>=0)tipMark(TIP_T);
}
function onTrack(e){var b=e.target.closest('[data-trk]');if(!b)return;var h=+b.getAttribute('data-trk');TRK=TRK===h?-1:h;TRKMAN=false;trackSort(true);cardSig='';renderCardSide(true);renderRack();renderMine();}
$('cardIn').addEventListener('click',onTrack);
/* ---------- tracking a hand sorts the rack for it: each group of the hand together (jokers in the groups they can fill), extras last ---------- */
var GAP={},EXTRA={},TRKSIG='',TRKMAN=false;
function trackSort(say){
  var ms=mySeat();GAP={};EXTRA={};TRKSIG='';
  if(!G||!G.seats||ms<0)return;var sd=G.seats[ms];
  if(TRK<0){ORDER=sortIds(sd.rack);rackSig='';return;}
  var cnt=counts(sd.rack),bi=null,bd=99;
  HANDS[TRK].inst.forEach(function(i){var d=dist(INST[i],cnt,sd.exp,null);if(d<bd){bd=d;bi=INST[i];}});
  if(!bi||bd>=40){ORDER=sortIds(sd.rack);rackSig='';if(say)toast('Can\u2019t make '+HANDS[TRK].name,'Your exposed tiles don\u2019t fit it',1800);return;}
  var m=expMatch(bi,sd.exp),pool=sortIds(sd.rack),used={},out=[],jk=pool.filter(function(id){return TT(id)===JK;});
  for(var g=0;g<bi.ts.length;g++){if(m&(1<<g))continue;var t=bi.ts[g],c=bi.cs[g],grp=[];
    pool.forEach(function(id){if(grp.length<c&&!used[id]&&TT(id)===t){grp.push(id);used[id]=1;}});
    if(c>=3)while(grp.length<c&&jk.length){var j=jk.shift();grp.push(j);used[j]=1;}
    if(grp.length){GAP[grp[0]]=1;out=out.concat(grp);}}
  var rest=pool.filter(function(id){return !used[id];});
  if(rest.length){GAP[rest[0]]=1;rest.forEach(function(id){EXTRA[id]=1;});}
  ORDER=out.concat(rest);rackSig='';TRKSIG=TRK+':'+sd.rack.slice().sort().join(',')+':'+sd.exp.length;
  if(say)toast('Sorted for '+HANDS[TRK].name,rest.length?'Its groups first \u00b7 dim tiles on the right don\u2019t fit':'Every tile fits!',1800);
}

/* ---------- tile tooltip: what the tile is, and which card hands (and which group in each) it counts for ---------- */
var TIP_T=-1,tipEl=null,tipHide=0,TIPMAP=null;
function tipMap(){if(TIPMAP)return TIPMAP;TIPMAP=[];for(var t=0;t<NT;t++){var m={};
  HANDS.forEach(function(h){var gs={};h.inst.forEach(function(n){var I=INST[n];for(var i=0;i<I.ts.length;i++)if(I.ts[i]===t)gs[i]=1;});var k=Object.keys(gs);if(k.length)m[h.i]=k.map(Number);});TIPMAP[t]=m;}
  return TIPMAP;}
var SUIT_DRAGON=['Soap (white)','Green','Red'];
function tipDesc(t){
  if(t<27){var su=(t/9)|0;return ['Dots','Bams','Craks'][su]+' suit \u00b7 its dragon is '+SUIT_DRAGON[su];}
  if(t<31)return 'Wind \u00b7 any \u25c7 on the card can be this';
  if(t===31)return 'Dragon \u00b7 goes with Craks';if(t===32)return 'Dragon \u00b7 goes with Bams';if(t===33)return 'White dragon \u00b7 goes with Dots';
  if(t===FL)return 'Fills the F spots on the card';
  return 'Wild: stands in for any tile in a group of 3 or more. Never in a pair or a single.';}
function tipMark(t){
  var map=t>=0&&t!==JK?tipMap()[t]:null;
  document.querySelectorAll('.mcard').forEach(function(mc){
    mc.classList.toggle('tipping',t>=0);
    mc.querySelectorAll('.hrow').forEach(function(r){var h=+r.getAttribute('data-trk'),gs=t===JK?null:map&&map[h];
      var on=t===JK?HANDS[h].gs.some(function(g){return g.c>=3;}):!!gs;
      r.classList.toggle('tipon',t>=0&&on);
      r.querySelectorAll('.pat i').forEach(function(g){var gi=+g.getAttribute('data-g');
        g.classList.toggle('gh',t>=0&&(t===JK?HANDS[h].gs[gi].c>=3:!!gs&&gs.indexOf(gi)>=0));});});
    if(t>=0){var first=mc.querySelector('.hrow.tipon'),box=mc.closest('#cardIn');
      if(first&&box){var br=box.getBoundingClientRect(),fr=first.getBoundingClientRect();if(fr.top<br.top||fr.bottom>br.bottom)box.scrollTop+=fr.top-br.top-40;}}
  });
}
function tipShow(el){
  var m=/\bty(\d+)\b/.exec(el.className||'');if(!m)return;var t=+m[1];clearTimeout(tipHide);
  if(!tipEl){tipEl=document.createElement('div');tipEl.className='mjtip';document.body.appendChild(tipEl);}
  var map=t===JK?null:tipMap()[t],n=t===JK?HANDS.filter(function(h){return h.gs.some(function(g){return g.c>=3;});}).length:Object.keys(map).length;
  var h='<b>'+tName(t)+'</b><i>'+tipDesc(t)+'</i>';
  var ms=mySeat(),near=ms>=0&&G&&G.seats&&G.phase!=='setup'?nearHands(ms):[],helps=[];
  if(t!==JK)near.forEach(function(x){if(map[x.h]&&x.d<=6&&helps.length<3)helps.push(esc(HANDS[x.h].name)+' <em>'+(x.d?x.d+' away':'ready')+'</em>');});
  if(TRK>=0&&t!==JK)h+='<u>'+(map[TRK]?'\u2714 Fits your tracked hand <em>'+esc(HANDS[TRK].name)+'</em>':'Not in your tracked hand '+esc(HANDS[TRK].name))+'</u>';
  else if(helps.length)h+='<u>Helps: '+helps.join(', ')+'</u>';
  h+='<u>Counts in '+n+' of '+HANDS.length+' hands on the card'+(felt.classList.contains('wide')?' (lit up on the card)':'')+'</u>';
  tipEl.innerHTML=h;tipEl.classList.add('on');
  var r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),tw=tipEl.offsetWidth,th=tipEl.offsetHeight;
  var x=Math.max(6,Math.min(innerWidth-tw-6,r.left+r.width/2-tw/2)),y=r.top-th-8;if(y<6)y=r.bottom+8;
  tipEl.style.left=x+'px';tipEl.style.top=y+'px';
  if(TIP_T!==t){TIP_T=t;tipMark(t);}
}
function tipOff(){clearTimeout(tipHide);if(tipEl)tipEl.classList.remove('on');if(TIP_T>=0){TIP_T=-1;tipMark(-1);}}
document.addEventListener('mouseover',function(e){var el=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.tile');if(el&&!el.classList.contains('tb')&&!el.closest('.mjtip'))tipShow(el);else if(TIP_T>=0&&!(e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.mcard')))tipOff();});
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){if(e.pointerType!=='touch')return;var el=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.tile');if(el&&!el.classList.contains('tb')){tipShow(el);clearTimeout(tipHide);tipHide=setTimeout(tipOff,2600);}},{passive:true});
addEventListener('scroll',function(e){if(e.target&&e.target.id==='cardIn')return;if(tipEl&&tipEl.classList.contains('on'))tipOff();},true);


/* =====================================================================
   Table state as this browser sees it
   ===================================================================== */
var G=null,ENG=true,DBG={auto:false,skill:'normal'},stepT=0,selfT=0,stepKey='',selfKey='',lastEvN=-1,lastGid='',bank=1000,record={w:0,l:0},best=0;
var ESC={gid:null,amt:0,done:false},OPP=[];
var SEL=[],ORDER=[],NEWT={},TRK=-1,handKey='',stageKey='';
function myId(){return LV.L?LV.L.me.id:'me';}
function myName(){return LV.mode==='solo'||!LV.L?'You':safeName(LV.L.me.name);}
function mySeat(){if(!G||!G.seats)return -1;for(var i=0;i<G.seats.length;i++){var sd=G.seats[i];if(sd.kind==='human'&&sd.id===myId())return i;}return -1;}
function viewSeat(){var m=mySeat();return m>=0?m:0;}
function nameOf(s){if(!G||!G.seats||!G.seats[s])return '';if(s===mySeat())return 'You';return safeName(G.seats[s].name);}
function seatCol(sd){if(sd.kind==='human')return '#22d3ee';var r=regOf(sd.name);return r?r[1]:'#c6a75e';}
function windOf(s){return G&&G.seats?['E','S','W','N'][(s-G.dealer+4)%4]:'';}
function posSeat(p){return (viewSeat()+p)%4;}
function idle(){return !G||!G.seats||G.phase==='over';}
function pickOpps(k){var pool=REG.slice(),out=[];while(out.length<k&&pool.length){out.push(pool.splice(rnd(pool.length),1)[0][0]);}return out;}
function fixOpps(){OPP=OPP.filter(function(n,i,a){return a.indexOf(n)===i&&regOf(n);}).slice(0,3);while(OPP.length<3){var pool=REG.filter(function(r){return OPP.indexOf(r[0])<0;});OPP.push(pool[rnd(pool.length)][0]);}}
function myCall(){var ms=mySeat();return G&&G.phase==='call'&&ms>=0&&G.elig.indexOf(ms)>=0&&!G.cl[ms];}
function myTurn(){var ms=mySeat();return !!G&&G.phase==='play'&&ms>=0&&G.turn===ms&&G.need==='discard'&&!LV.sent;}
function myPending(){var ms=mySeat();return ms>=0&&!LV.sent&&pending(G).indexOf(ms)>=0;}
function swapOpts(s){
  var out=[];if(!G||G.phase!=='play'||G.turn!==s||G.seats[s].dead)return out;var rk=G.seats[s].rack;
  G.seats.forEach(function(p,pi){p.exp.forEach(function(e,ei){if(!e.ids.some(function(id){return TT(id)===JK;}))return;rk.forEach(function(id){if(TT(id)===e.t)out.push({id:id,p:pi,e:ei});});});});
  return out;
}

/* =====================================================================
   Layout: the table's size comes from the viewport only; tiles scale inside it
   ===================================================================== */
var BW=600,BH=500,PR={x:0,y:0,w:100,h:100};
function layoutStage(){
  BW=stage.clientWidth;BH=stage.clientHeight;if(BW<20)return;
  var ph=BW<620;stage.classList.toggle('ph',ph);
  var sw=ph?Math.max(66,Math.round(BW*0.18)):Math.round(clamp(BW*0.17,140,230));
  var et=ph?12:clamp(Math.floor((sw-14)/6)-1,15,28);
  var eta=ph?clamp(Math.floor((BW-160)/17),11,15):clamp(Math.floor((BW-2*sw-60)/17),16,28);
  stage.classList.toggle('lg',BW>=1100&&BH>=560);
  stage.style.setProperty('--hb',Math.min(et*0.62,(sw-16)/14-1).toFixed(1)+'px');stage.style.setProperty('--hba',Math.min(eta*0.62,16).toFixed(1)+'px');
  stage.style.setProperty('--sw',sw+'px');stage.style.setProperty('--et',et+'px');stage.style.setProperty('--eta',eta+'px');
  var topH=(ph?46:BW>=1100&&BH>=560?84:62)+eta*1.36+12;
  PR={x:sw+14,y:Math.round(topH+6),w:BW-2*(sw+14),h:0};PR.h=BH-PR.y-10;
  var pool=$('pool');pool.style.left=PR.x+'px';pool.style.top=PR.y+'px';pool.style.width=PR.w+'px';pool.style.height=PR.h+'px';
  sizePool();
}
function sizePool(){
  var n=Math.max(G&&G.dis?G.dis.length:0,64),W=PR.w,H=PR.h-30,best=10;
  for(var w=Math.min(48,Math.round(BW/26));w>=9;w--){var cols=Math.floor((W+2)/(w+2));if(cols<1)continue;var rows=Math.ceil(n/cols);if(rows*(w*1.36+2)<=H){best=w;break;}}
  $('pin').style.setProperty('--pt',best+'px');
}
function fit(){
  var fs=root.classList.contains('fs'),short=root.classList.contains('fsshort');
  var fw=$('fin').clientWidth;if(fw<20)return;
  var wide=fw>=1080&&!short;felt.classList.toggle('wide',wide);
  var mw=wide?fw-330-12:fw;
  var per,rt,mt;
  if(short){per=14;rt=clamp(Math.floor((mw-13*4-8)/14),24,44);}
  else if(mw>=14*44+13*5){per=14;rt=clamp(Math.floor((mw-13*5-16)/14),40,fs?66:60);}
  else{per=7;rt=clamp(Math.floor((mw-6*5-8)/7),34,52);}
  var rg=short?4:5;
  mt=short?clamp(Math.round(rt*0.5),15,22):per===7?clamp(Math.floor((mw-110)/16),15,22):clamp(Math.round(rt*0.56),20,34);
  root.style.setProperty('--rt',rt+'px');root.style.setProperty('--per',per);root.style.setProperty('--rg',rg+'px');root.style.setProperty('--rows',per===7?2:1);root.style.setProperty('--mt',mt+'px');
  if(!fs){var h=mw<620?Math.round(clamp(mw*1.12,380,540)):Math.round(clamp(mw*0.54,400,600));stage.style.height=h+'px';}
  else stage.style.height='';
  layoutStage();
  if(G&&G.seats){renderPool();}
  renderCardSide();
}
var fitRaf=0;function refit(){cancelAnimationFrame(fitRaf);fitRaf=requestAnimationFrame(fit);}
addEventListener('resize',refit);
addEventListener('message',function(e){var d=e.data;if(d&&d.arcade==='fs')setTimeout(fit,40);});
if(window.ResizeObserver){try{new ResizeObserver(refit).observe($('fin'));new ResizeObserver(function(){layoutStage();}).observe(stage);}catch(e){}}

var seatSig={};
function expHTML(sd,s,swp){
  return sd.exp.map(function(e,i){var sw=swp&&swp.some(function(o){return o.p===s&&o.e===i;});
    return '<span class="eg" data-s="'+s+'" data-e="'+i+'">'+e.ids.map(function(id){return tileH(id,sw&&TT(id)===JK?'sw':'');}).join('')+'</span>';}).join('');
}
function renderSeats(){
  var ms=mySeat(),swp=myTurn()?swapOpts(ms):null;
  [['r',1],['a',2],['l',3]].forEach(function(pp){
    var el=$('seat-'+pp[0]);
    if(!G||!G.seats){
      fixOpps();var nm=OPP[pp[1]-1],r=regOf(nm),sig0='pre:'+nm;
      if(seatSig[pp[0]]!==sig0){seatSig[pp[0]]=sig0;el.innerHTML=plateHTML(nm,r?r[1]:'#c6a75e');}
      el.querySelector('.c').textContent='13';el.querySelector('.pt').textContent='0';el.querySelector('.wd').textContent='';el.querySelector('.ex').innerHTML='';
      el.querySelector('.plate').className='plate';return;}
    var s=posSeat(pp[1]),sd=G.seats[s],sig=s+':'+sd.name+':'+sd.kind;
    if(seatSig[pp[0]]!==sig){seatSig[pp[0]]=sig;el.innerHTML=plateHTML(safeName(sd.name),seatCol(sd));el.setAttribute('data-s',s);}
    el.querySelector('.c').textContent=String(sd.rack.length);
    var hd=el.querySelector('.hid');if(hd.childElementCount!==sd.rack.length)hd.innerHTML=new Array(sd.rack.length+1).join('<span class="tile tb"></span>');
    var pe=el.querySelector('.pt');pe.textContent=pts(sd.pts);pe.classList.toggle('neg',sd.pts<0);
    var wd=el.querySelector('.wd');wd.textContent=windOf(s);wd.classList.toggle('e',windOf(s)==='E');
    var pl=el.querySelector('.plate');
    pl.classList.toggle('turn',(G.phase==='play'&&G.turn===s)||(G.phase==='char'&&pending(G).indexOf(s)>=0)||(G.phase==='call'&&G.elig.indexOf(s)>=0&&!G.cl[s]));
    pl.classList.toggle('won',(G.phase==='round'||G.phase==='over')&&G.res&&G.res.w===s);
    pl.classList.toggle('dead',!!sd.dead);
    var ex=el.querySelector('.ex'),esig=sd.exp.map(function(e){return e.ids.join('.');}).join('|')+(swp?JSON.stringify(swp):'');
    if(ex.getAttribute('data-sig')!==esig){var before=ex.children.length;ex.setAttribute('data-sig',esig);ex.innerHTML=expHTML(sd,s,swp);if(ex.children.length>before&&before>=0&&ex.lastChild)ex.lastChild.classList.add('pop');}
  });
}
function plateHTML(nm,col){return '<div class="hid"></div><div class="plate"><div class="av" style="background:'+col+'"><span>'+esc(nm.charAt(0).toUpperCase())+'</span></div><div class="pi"><div class="nm">'+esc(nm)+'</div><div class="ps"><span class="wd"></span><span class="cnt"><i></i><b class="c">13</b></span><b class="pt">0</b></div></div><span class="tmr"></span></div><div class="ex"></div><div class="talk"></div>';}
var poolSig='';
function renderPool(){
  var pin=$('pin');
  if(!G||!G.seats){pin.innerHTML='';poolSig='';$('ann').innerHTML='';return;}
  var sig=G.gid+':'+G.hand+':'+G.dis.length+':'+(G.last?G.last.id:'');
  if(sig!==poolSig){var prev=poolSig.split(':'),grew=prev[0]===G.gid&&+prev[1]===G.hand&&G.dis.length>+prev[2];poolSig=sig;sizePool();
    pin.innerHTML=G.dis.map(function(id,i){var last=G.last&&G.last.id===id&&i===G.dis.length-1;return tileH(id,(last?'last':'')+(grew&&i===G.dis.length-1?' pop':'')+(TT(id)===JK?' dimj':''));}).join('');}
  /* the announcer line */
  var a='',ev=G.ev;
  if(G.phase==='char'){var st=chStage(G);a=st.v?'Second Charleston?':(st.c?'Courtesy pass &middot; across':'Charleston &middot; pass <b>'+st.nm+'</b>');}
  else if(G.last&&(G.phase==='call'||G.phase==='play')&&G.dis.length){a=tileH(G.last.id)+'<span><b>'+esc(nameOf(G.last.by))+'</b> '+(G.last.by===mySeat()?'threw':'threw')+' '+tName(TT(G.last.id))+'</span>';}
  else if(G.phase==='play'&&G.dis.length){var lid=G.dis[G.dis.length-1];a='<span>Last thrown: '+tName(TT(lid))+'</span>';}
  else if(G.phase==='play')a='<span><b>'+esc(nameOf(G.dealer))+'</b> (East) opens</span>';
  else if(G.phase==='round'||G.phase==='over')a=G.res&&G.res.w>=0?'<span><b>'+esc(nameOf(G.res.w))+'</b> &middot; Mah Jongg!</span>':'<span>Wall game</span>';
  if($('ann').getAttribute('data-h')!==a){$('ann').setAttribute('data-h',a);$('ann').innerHTML=a;}
}
var calloutOn=false;
function renderCallout(){
  var co=$('callout'),on=!!(myCall()&&G.last);
  if(on){var sig='c'+G.evn;if(co.getAttribute('data-sig')!==sig){co.setAttribute('data-sig',sig);var o=callOpts(G,mySeat());
    co.innerHTML=tileH(G.last.id)+'<div class="ct"><b>'+esc(nameOf(G.last.by))+'</b> threw '+tName(TT(G.last.id))+'.<br>'+(o.mj?'It wins you the hand!':'You can call it.')+'</div><div class="cbar"><i></i></div>';}}
  co.classList.toggle('on',on);calloutOn=on;
}
function hintIds(){
  if(TUT.on&&Object.keys(TUT.glow).length){var tg={};for(var k in TUT.glow)tg[k]=1;return tg;}
  var ms=mySeat();if(!opt.hints||!G||!G.seats||ms<0||G.phase==='round'||G.phase==='over')return {};
  var sd=G.seats[ms],cnt=counts(sd.rack),bi=null,bd=99;
  var list=TRK>=0?HANDS[TRK].inst:null;
  if(list)list.forEach(function(i){var d=dist(INST[i],cnt,sd.exp,null);if(d<bd){bd=d;bi=INST[i];}});
  else{var t=topInsts(cnt,sd.exp,null,-1,1);if(t.length){bi=INST[t[0].i];bd=t[0].d;}}
  var out={};if(!bi||bd>=40)return out;
  var m=expMatch(bi,sd.exp);if(m<0)return out;var need={};
  for(var g=0;g<bi.ts.length;g++)if(!(m&(1<<g)))need[bi.ts[g]]=bi.cs[g];
  ORDER.forEach(function(id){var t=TT(id);if(t===JK){out[id]=1;return;}if(need[t]>0){need[t]--;out[id]=1;}});
  out._h=bi.h;out._d=bd;return out;
}
var rackSig='';
function renderRack(fresh){
  var rin=$('rin'),ms=mySeat();
  if(!G||!G.seats){rin.innerHTML='';rackSig='';return;}
  if(ms<0){var vs=viewSeat(),n=G.seats[vs].rack.length,s0='watch:'+n;if(rackSig!==s0){rackSig=s0;rin.innerHTML='<div class="watch">'+new Array(n+1).join('<span class="tile tb"></span>')+'<br>You&rsquo;re watching this table. You take a seat at the next deal.</div>';}return;}
  syncOrder();
  var hi=hintIds(),sig=Object.keys(GAP).join('.')+'/'+Object.keys(EXTRA).length+'|'+ORDER.join(',')+'|'+SEL.join(',')+'|'+Object.keys(NEWT).join(',')+'|'+Object.keys(hi).join(',')+(fresh?'|f':'');
  if(sig===rackSig&&!fresh)return;rackSig=sig;
  rin.innerHTML=ORDER.map(function(id,i){var t=TT(id);return '<button type="button" class="tile rkt ty'+t+(t===JK?' jk':'')+(SEL.indexOf(id)>=0?' sel':'')+(NEWT[id]?' newt':'')+(hi[id]?' hint':'')+(GAP[id]&&i>0?' gs':'')+(EXTRA[id]?' xt':'')+(TUT.on&&TUT.glow[id]?' tutg':'')+(fresh?' dealt':'')+'" data-id="'+id+'" aria-label="'+tName(t)+'"'+(fresh?' style="animation-delay:'+Math.round(i*D(35))+'ms"':'')+'></button>';}).join('');
}
function syncOrder(){
  var ms=mySeat();if(!G||!G.seats||ms<0){ORDER=[];SEL=[];return;}
  var rack=G.seats[ms].rack,set={};rack.forEach(function(id){set[id]=1;});
  var hk=G.gid+':'+G.hand;
  if(hk!==handKey){handKey=hk;ORDER=sortIds(rack);SEL=[];NEWT={};TRK=-1;cardSig='';}
  ORDER=ORDER.filter(function(id){return set[id];});var have={};ORDER.forEach(function(id){have[id]=1;});rack.forEach(function(id){if(!have[id])ORDER.push(id);});
  SEL=SEL.filter(function(id){return set[id];});
  for(var k in NEWT)if(!set[k])delete NEWT[k];
  if(TRK>=0&&!TRKMAN){var ts=TRK+':'+rack.slice().sort().join(',')+':'+G.seats[ms].exp.length;if(ts!==TRKSIG)trackSort(false);}
  else if(TRK<0&&(Object.keys(GAP).length)){GAP={};EXTRA={};}
}
function sortIds(a){return a.slice().sort(function(x,y){return TT(x)-TT(y)||x-y;});}
var mexpSig='';
