  var ms=mySeat(),vs=viewSeat(),sd=G&&G.seats?G.seats[vs]:null;
  $('nmMe').textContent=ms>=0||!sd?myName():safeName(sd.name);$('avMe').firstChild.textContent=($('nmMe').textContent||'Y').charAt(0).toUpperCase();
  $('avMe').style.background=ms>=0||!sd?'#10b981':seatCol(sd);
  var pe=$('ptMe');pe.textContent=sd?pts(sd.pts):'0';pe.classList.toggle('neg',!!sd&&sd.pts<0);
  var wd=$('wdMe');wd.textContent=sd?windOf(vs):'E';wd.classList.toggle('e',sd?windOf(vs)==='E':true);
  var p=$('pMe');
  p.classList.toggle('turn',!!sd&&((G.phase==='play'&&G.turn===vs)||(G.phase==='char'&&pending(G).indexOf(vs)>=0)||(G.phase==='call'&&G.elig.indexOf(vs)>=0&&!G.cl[vs])));
  p.classList.toggle('won',!!sd&&(G.phase==='round'||G.phase==='over')&&G.res&&G.res.w===vs);
  p.classList.toggle('dead',!!sd&&sd.dead);
  var me=$('mexp'),swp=myTurn()?swapOpts(ms):null,h;
  if(!sd)h='<span class="none">Your exposures show here.</span>';
  else if(sd.exp.length)h=expHTML(sd,vs,swp);
  else{var hi=ms>=0?hintIds():{},hn=hi._h!==undefined?HANDS[hi._h]:null;
    h='<span class="none">'+(hn?(TRK>=0?'Tracking ':'Closest: ')+'<b>'+esc(hn.name)+'</b> &middot; '+(hi._d?hi._d+' away':'ready'):'No exposures yet')+'</span>';}
  if(h!==mexpSig){var grew=sd&&sd.exp.length&&me.querySelectorAll('.eg').length<sd.exp.length;mexpSig=h;me.innerHTML=h;if(grew&&me.lastChild)me.lastChild.classList.add('pop');}
}
function hud(){
  $('sBank').textContent=money(bank);
  var ms=mySeat(),sd=ms>=0?G.seats[ms]:null;
  $('sPts').textContent=sd?pts(sd.pts):'0';
  $('sHand').textContent=G&&G.seats?Math.min(G.hand,G.hands)+'/'+G.hands:opt.hands+' hands';
  $('sPot').textContent=ESC.amt&&!ESC.done?money(ESC.amt*4):(G&&G.seats&&G.phase!=='over'?'Fun':'\u2013');
  $('wallN').textContent=G&&G.wall?String(G.wall.length):'\u2013';
  $('handN').textContent=G&&G.seats?Math.min(G.hand,G.hands)+' / '+G.hands:'\u2013';
}

/* ---------- action buttons ---------- */
var actSig='';
function actList(){
  var ms=mySeat(),L=[];
  if(!G||!G.seats){L.push(LV.mode==='guest'?{a:'',t:'Waiting\u2026',c:'gold',d:1}:{a:'deal',t:'Deal',c:'gold',k:1});return L;}
  if(G.phase==='round'){L.push({a:'next',t:'Next hand',c:'gold',d:LV.mode==='guest',k:1});return L;}
  if(G.phase==='over'){L.push({a:'again',t:LV.mode==='guest'?'Waiting\u2026':'New game',c:'gold',d:LV.mode==='guest',k:1});return L;}
  if(ms<0){L.push({a:'',t:'Watching',c:'gold',d:1});return L;}
  if(LV.sent){L.push({a:'',t:'Sending\u2026',c:'gold',d:1});return L;}
  if(G.phase==='char'){
    var st=chStage(G);
    if(st.v){if(G.ch.votes[ms]===null){L.push({a:'vote0',t:'Stop',c:'btn2'});L.push({a:'vote1',t:'Keep passing',c:'gold',k:1});}else L.push({a:'',t:'Waiting\u2026',c:'gold',d:1});return L;}
    if(G.ch.picks[ms]){L.push({a:'',t:'Waiting\u2026',c:'gold',d:1});return L;}
    L.push({a:'suggest',t:'Suggest',c:'btn2'});
    if(st.c)L.push({a:'pass',t:SEL.length?'Pass '+SEL.length:'Skip',c:'gold',k:1});
    else L.push({a:'pass',t:'Pass 3',c:'gold',d:SEL.length!==3,k:1});
    return L;
  }
  if(G.phase==='call'){
    if(myCall()){var o=callOpts(G,ms);if(o.mj)L.push({a:'cmj',t:'Mah Jongg!',c:'mjb'});
      o.c.forEach(function(c){L.push({a:'c'+c,t:{3:'Pung',4:'Kong',5:'Quint'}[c],c:'btn'});});L.push({a:'nocall',t:'Pass',c:'btn2',k:1});return L;}
    L.push({a:'',t:G.cl&&G.cl[ms]&&!G.cl[ms].no?'Calling\u2026':'Waiting\u2026',c:'gold',d:1});return L;
  }
  if(G.phase==='play'){
    if(G.turn===ms&&G.need==='discard'){
      if(findWin(G.seats[ms].rack,G.seats[ms].exp)>=0)L.push({a:'mj',t:'Mah Jongg!',c:'mjb'});
      if(swapOpts(ms).length)L.push({a:'swap',t:'Swap joker',c:'btn2'});
      L.push({a:'discard',t:'Throw',c:'gold',d:SEL.length!==1,k:1});return L;}
    L.push({a:'',t:G.seats[ms].dead?'Dead hand':(nameOf(G.turn)+'\u2019s turn'),c:'gold',d:1});return L;
  }
  return L;
}
function renderActs(){
  var L=actList(),sig=JSON.stringify(L)+(TUT.on?TUT.btn:'');if(sig===actSig)return;actSig=sig;
  $('acts').innerHTML=L.map(function(b){var cls=b.c==='btn2'?'btn2':b.c==='mjb'?'btn mjb':b.c==='btn'?'btn':'btn gold';
    return '<button type="button" class="'+cls+(TUT.on&&TUT.btn&&TUT.btn===b.a?' tutb':'')+'" data-a="'+b.a+'"'+(b.d?' disabled':'')+'>'+esc(b.t)+(b.k&&!b.d?' <kbd>Space</kbd>':'')+'</button>';}).join('');
}
function renderAll(fresh){renderSeats();renderMine();renderPool();renderRack(fresh);renderCallout();renderActs();hud();renderScores();renderCardSide();}

/* ---------- table talk ---------- */
var talkT={};
function seatEl(s){var vs=viewSeat();if(s===vs)return $('pMe');var p=(s-vs+4)%4;return $('seat-'+['','r','a','l'][p]);}
function botSay(s,kind,p,vars){
  if(!opt.talk||!G||LV.mode==='guest'||!G.seats[s]||G.seats[s].kind!=='bot')return;if(Math.random()>(p===undefined?0.5:p))return;
  var txt=pick(LINES[kind]);if(vars)for(var k in vars)txt=txt.replace('{'+k+'}',vars[k]);
  bubble(s,txt);
  try{if(LV.chat){var sd=G.seats[s];LV.chat.post('bot:'+sd.name.toLowerCase(),sd.name,txt,seatCol(sd));}}catch(e){}
}
function bubble(s,txt){var el=seatEl(s);if(!el)return;var t=el.querySelector('.talk');if(!t)return;t.textContent=txt;t.classList.add('on');clearTimeout(talkT[s]);talkT[s]=setTimeout(function(){t.classList.remove('on');},2800);}

/* ---------- log + scores ---------- */
function logLine(t,c){var li=document.createElement('li');if(c)li.className=c;li.innerHTML=t;var L0=$('log');L0.insertBefore(li,L0.firstChild);while(L0.children.length>180)L0.removeChild(L0.lastChild);}
function scoreTable(withHand){
  if(!G||!G.seats)return '';var ms=mySeat(),res=G.res||null,h='<table class="sc-t"><tr><th>Player</th>'+(withHand?'<th>This hand</th>':'')+'<th>Total</th></tr>';
  var order=[0,1,2,3].sort(function(a,b){return G.seats[b].pts-G.seats[a].pts;});
  order.forEach(function(i){var sd=G.seats[i],w=G.phase==='over'?G.win===i:res&&res.w===i,p=res?res.pay[i]:0;
    h+='<tr class="'+(w?'w ':'')+(i===ms?'me':'')+'"><td>'+esc(i===ms?'You':safeName(sd.name))+(sd.kind==='human'&&i!==ms?' <small style="color:#22d3ee">live</small>':'')+(sd.dead?' <small style="color:#fca5a5">dead</small>':'')+'</td>'+
      (withHand?'<td class="'+(p<0?'neg':'')+'">'+(res?pts(p):'\u2013')+'</td>':'')+'<td class="'+(sd.pts<0?'neg':'')+'">'+pts(sd.pts)+'</td></tr>';});
  return h+'</table>';
}
var scoreSig='';
function renderScores(){var sig=G&&G.seats?G.gid+':'+G.hist.length+':'+G.seats.map(function(x){return x.name+x.pts;}).join():'';if(sig===scoreSig)return;scoreSig=sig;
  $('scores').innerHTML=G&&G.seats?scoreTable(!!G.hist.length)+'<p style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;margin:0">'+G.hands+' hands &middot; points settle each hand.</p>':'<p style="color:var(--muted);font-size:13px;margin:0">No game yet.</p>';}

/* =====================================================================
   Turn engine (solo + live host): computer players, call windows, turn clocks
   ===================================================================== */
var TURN_MS=30000,CALL_MS=3000,CH_MS=40000;
function evKey(){return G?G.gid+':'+G.hand+':'+G.evn+':'+G.phase:'';}
function skillOf(s){return G.seats[s].skill||opt.skill;}
function stKey(){return G?G.phase+':'+G.turn+':'+(G.ch?G.ch.i:'')+':'+G.hand:'';}
function cancelSteps(){clearTimeout(stepT);clearTimeout(selfT);stepKey='';selfKey='';}
function runStep(){
  if(!ENG||!G||!G.seats)return;
  if(TUT.on&&TUT.hold)return;
  var key=evKey();if(key===stepKey)return;clearTimeout(stepT);stepKey=key;
  if(G.phase==='round'){if(DBG.auto||LV.mode==='host')stepT=later(DBG.auto?D(900):9000,function(){if(G&&G.phase==='round'&&ENG)nextHand();});return;}
  if(G.phase!=='char'&&G.phase!=='play'&&G.phase!=='call')return;
  var pend=pending(G),bots=pend.filter(function(s){return G.seats[s].kind==='bot';}),people=pend.filter(function(s){return G.seats[s].kind!=='bot';});
  if(G.phase==='call'){
    if(bots.length){stepT=later(D(220),function(){if(evKey()!==key)return;doActs(bots.map(function(s){return [s,botPlay(s)];}));});return;}
    if(people.length&&!G.cdl&&!TUT.on){G.cdl=Date.now()+(LV.mode==='solo'?CALL_MS:CALL_MS+700);publish();}
    return;
  }
  if(G.phase==='char'){
    if(bots.length)stepT=later(D(650+R()*450),function(){if(evKey()!==key)return;doActs(bots.map(function(s){return [s,botPlay(s)];}));});
    if(people.length&&LV.mode!=='solo'&&!G.dl){G.dl=Date.now()+CH_MS;publish();}
    return;
  }
  var s=G.turn;
  if(G.seats[s].kind==='bot'){stepT=later(D(700+R()*600),function(){if(evKey()!==key)return;doAct(s,botPlay(s));});return;}
  if(LV.mode!=='solo'&&!G.dl){G.dl=Date.now()+TURN_MS;publish();}
}
/* my own seat on autoplay (tests) */
function selfStep(){
  if(!DBG.auto||!G||!G.seats||!myPending())return;
  var key=evKey();if(key===selfKey)return;clearTimeout(selfT);selfKey=key;var ms=mySeat();
  selfT=later(D(G.phase==='call'?150:G.phase==='char'?400:330),function(){if(!DBG.auto||evKey()!==key||!myPending())return;var c=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(G));myAct(botAct(c,ms,DBG.skill));});
}
function myAct(a){
  var s=mySeat();if(s<0||!G||!a)return false;
  if(TUT.on&&G.gid===TUT.gid&&tutBlock(a))return false;
  if(LV.mode==='guest'){
    var test=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(G));if(!apply(test,s,a))return false;
    LV.sent=true;SEL=[];LV.L.send('act',{gid:G.gid,n:G.evn,a:a},LV.hostId);
    clearTimeout(LV.sentT);LV.sentT=setTimeout(function(){if(LV.sent){LV.sent=false;selfKey='';renderAll();status();selfStep();}},4000);
    renderAll();status();return true;
  }
  return doAct(s,a);
}
function doAct(s,a){return doActs([[s,a]]);}
function doActs(list){
  if(!G)return false;var k0=stKey(),any=false;
  list.forEach(function(x){if(!G||!x[1])return;if(x[0]>=0&&pending(G).indexOf(x[0])<0&&!(G.phase==='play'&&x[0]===G.turn))return;if(apply(G,x[0],x[1]))any=true;});
  if(!any)return false;
  if(G.phase!=='call')G.cdl=0;
  if(stKey()!==k0)G.dl=0;
  publish();show();runStep();return true;
}
function nextHand(){
  if(!G||G.phase!=='round')return;cancelSteps();
  if(LV.mode==='host'){var seated={};G.seats.forEach(function(sd){if(sd.kind==='human')seated[sd.id]=1;});
    LV.roster.forEach(function(p){if(seated[p.id])return;for(var i=0;i<4;i++){var sd=G.seats[i];if(sd.kind==='bot'&&!sd.was){sd.kind='human';sd.id=p.id;sd.name=safeName(p.name);seated[p.id]=1;logLine(esc(sd.name)+' takes a seat','h');break;}}});}
  dealHand(G,RNG);G.dl=0;G.cdl=0;sheetUser='';
  publish();show();runStep();
}

/* what this browser shows after any change (engine or host message) */
function show(){
  if(!G||!G.seats){renderAll();status();syncSheet();return;}
  if(G.gid!==lastGid){lastGid=G.gid;lastEvN=G.evs&&G.evs.length?G.evs[0].n-1:0;$('log').innerHTML='';}
  var fresh=false,ms=mySeat();
  (G.evs||[]).forEach(function(ev){if(ev.n<=lastEvN)return;lastEvN=ev.n;if(ev.kind==='deal')fresh=true;narrate(ev);});
  var k=stKey();if(k!==stageKey){stageKey=k;SEL=[];}
  if(fresh){NEWT={};handKey='';}
  renderAll(fresh);
  status();checkMoney();syncSheet();selfStep();tutTick();
}
function status(){
  var m=$('msg');function say(h){if(m.getAttribute('data-h')!==h){m.setAttribute('data-h',h);m.innerHTML=h;}}
  if(!G||!G.seats){say(LV.mode==='guest'?'Waiting for the host to deal\u2026':'Pick your table to start.');return;}
  var ms=mySeat();
  if(G.phase==='round'||G.phase==='over'){var r=G.res;say(r&&r.w>=0?'<b>'+esc(nameOf(r.w))+'</b> '+(r.w===ms?'win':'wins')+' with '+esc(HANDS[r.h].name)+' &middot; '+r.val+(r.jl?' (jokerless)':''):'Wall game. Nobody wins this hand.');return;}
  if(ms<0){say('Watching: '+esc(nameOf(G.turn))+'\u2019s turn.');return;}
  if(LV.sent){say('Sending\u2026');return;}
  var sd=G.seats[ms];
  if(G.phase==='char'){
    var st=chStage(G),wait=pending(G).filter(function(s){return s!==ms;}).map(function(s){return esc(nameOf(s));});
    if(st.v){say(G.ch.votes[ms]===null?'Second Charleston? <b>Everyone</b> has to agree.':'Waiting for '+wait.join(', ')+'\u2026');return;}
    if(G.ch.picks[ms]){say('Waiting for '+wait.join(', ')+'\u2026');return;}
    var to=esc(nameOf((ms+st.d)%4));
    say(st.c?'Courtesy: up to <b>3</b> tiles across to '+to+'.':'Pick <b>3</b> to pass <b>'+st.nm+'</b> to '+to+'. No jokers.');return;}
  if(sd.dead){say('Your hand is <b>dead</b>. Sit tight until the next hand.');return;}
  if(G.phase==='call'){if(myCall()){var o=callOpts(G,ms);say(o.mj?'<b>Mah Jongg</b> on that tile!':'Call the <b>'+tName(TT(G.last.id))+'</b>?');}else say(esc(nameOf(G.last.by))+' threw '+tName(TT(G.last.id))+'.');return;}
  if(G.phase==='play'){
    if(G.turn===ms){
      if(findWin(sd.rack,sd.exp)>=0){say('You have <b>Mah Jongg</b>! Declare it.');return;}
      var dr=G.drawn>=0&&sd.rack.indexOf(G.drawn)>=0?'You drew <b>'+tName(TT(G.drawn))+'</b>. ':'';
      say(dr+(SEL.length===1?'Tap it again or press <b>Throw</b>.':'Your turn: pick a tile to throw.'));return;}
    say(esc(nameOf(G.turn))+' is thinking\u2026');return;}
}
function narrate(ev){
  if(!ev||!G)return;var s=ev.seat,n=s>=0?esc(nameOf(s)):'',ms=mySeat(),me=s===ms;
  if(ev.kind==='deal'){logLine('Hand '+G.hand+' of '+G.hands+' &middot; '+esc(nameOf(G.dealer))+' is East','h');SFX.shuffle();if(G.hand===1)for(var i=0;i<4;i++)if(G.seats[i].kind==='bot'){botSay(i,'hello',0.6);break;}}
  else if(ev.kind==='passed'){SFX.pass();logLine(ev.c?'Courtesy pass across':'Charleston: everyone passes '+ev.nm,'');
    if(ms>=0&&ev.got&&ev.got[ms]){ev.got[ms].forEach(function(id){NEWT[id]=1;});if(ev.got[ms].length)toast('You got '+ev.got[ms].length+' tile'+(ev.got[ms].length>1?'s':''),'from '+nameOf((ms+4-ev.d)%4),1300);}
    if(!ev.c&&Math.random()<0.3){var b=[0,1,2,3].filter(function(x){return G.seats[x].kind==='bot';});if(b.length)botSay(pick(b),'pass',0.6);}}
  else if(ev.kind==='voted')logLine(ev.go?'Second Charleston':'Charleston stopped','');
  else if(ev.kind==='play'){logLine(n+' (East) opens the play','');}
  else if(ev.kind==='discard'){SFX.clack();logLine('<b>'+n+'</b> '+(me?'throw':'throws')+' '+tName(TT(ev.id)),me?'w':'');}
  else if(ev.kind==='draw'){if(me){SFX.draw();NEWT={};NEWT[ev.id]=1;setTimeout(SFX.turn,120);}}
  else if(ev.kind==='call'){
    if(ev.mj){logLine('<b>'+n+'</b> '+(ev.self?'draws':'calls')+' Mah Jongg'+(ev.self?'':' on '+esc(nameOf(ev.by))+'\u2019s '+tName(TT(ev.id))),'w');}
    else{SFX.call();var nmx={3:'Pung',4:'Kong',5:'Quint'}[ev.c];logLine('<b>'+n+'</b> '+(me?'call':'calls')+' '+nmx+' of '+tName(ev.t),me?'w':'');
      if(G.seats[s].kind==='bot')botSay(s,pick([nmx.toLowerCase(),'call']),0.75);else if(!me)bubble(s,nmx+'!');
      toast(nameOf(s)+(me?' call ':' calls ')+nmx+'!',tName(ev.t),1200);}}
  else if(ev.kind==='swap'){SFX.swap();logLine('<b>'+n+'</b> '+(me?'swap':'swaps')+' a '+tName(TT(ev.id))+' for '+(ev.p===s?'their own':esc(nameOf(ev.p))+'\u2019s')+' joker','');botSay(s,'swap',0.6);}
  else if(ev.kind==='dead'){logLine(n+'\u2019s hand is dead','l');if(me)toast('Your hand is dead','Those exposures can\u2019t make any hand now',2600);else botSay(s,'dead',0.8);}
  else if(ev.kind==='mj'||ev.kind==='wall')onHandEnd();
}
function onHandEnd(){
  var r=G.res,ms=mySeat();if(!r)return;
  if(r.w<0){SFX.wall();logLine('Wall game &ndash; nobody wins','l');var b=[0,1,2,3].filter(function(x){return G.seats[x].kind==='bot';});if(b.length)botSay(pick(b),'wall',0.6);}
  else{var h=HANDS[r.h];logLine(esc(nameOf(r.w))+' &middot; '+esc(h.name)+' ('+r.val+(r.jl?', jokerless':'')+') '+(r.how==='self'?'self-drawn':'off '+esc(nameOf(r.by))),r.w===ms?'w':'l');
    if(r.w===ms){SFX.win();if(typeof suBanner==='function')suBanner('Mah Jongg!',pts(r.pay[ms])+' points \u00b7 '+h.name);}else{if(ms>=0)SFX.lose();botSay(r.w,'mj',0.9);}
    TRK=-1;cardSig='';}
  if(G.phase==='over'){var iw=G.win===ms;logLine((iw?'You win':esc(nameOf(G.win))+' wins')+' the game','h');
    if(G.seats[G.win].kind==='bot')botSay(G.win,'win',0.8);else{for(var i=0;i<4;i++)if(G.seats[i].kind==='bot'){botSay(i,'lose',0.7);break;}}}
}
function toast(t,sub,ms){var el=$('toast');el.innerHTML=esc(t)+(sub?'<small>'+esc(sub)+'</small>':'');el.classList.add('on');clearTimeout(toast.t);toast.t=setTimeout(function(){el.classList.remove('on');},ms||1600);}

/* stakes: everyone puts the stake in the pot; at the end the points decide each share of it */
function shares(g){
  if(!g.stake)return null;var st=g.stake,pot=st*4,rate=st/200,v=g.seats.map(function(x){return Math.max(0,st+x.pts*rate);}),sum=v.reduce(function(a,b){return a+b;},0)||1;
  return v.map(function(x){return Math.round(pot*x/sum*100)/100;});
}
function checkMoney(){
  if(!G||!G.seats||TUT.on){hud();return;}
  var ms=mySeat();
  if(ESC.gid!==G.gid){
    if(ESC.amt&&!ESC.done){bank+=ESC.amt;toast('Game cancelled','Your stake is back in your bankroll',2400);}
    ESC={gid:G.gid,amt:0,done:false,share:0};
    if(ms>=0&&G.stake&&G.hand===1&&!G.hist.length){if(bank>=G.stake){bank-=G.stake;ESC.amt=G.stake;}else toast('Playing for fun','Your bankroll can\u2019t cover the stake');}
  }
  if(G.phase==='over'&&!ESC.done){ESC.done=true;
    if(ms>=0){var iw=G.win===ms;if(iw)record.w++;else record.l++;
      if(ESC.amt){var sh=shares(G);ESC.share=sh?sh[ms]:0;bank+=ESC.share;if(ESC.share-ESC.amt>best)best=ESC.share-ESC.amt;}
      if(iw&&typeof suBanner==='function')setTimeout(function(){suBanner('You Win!',ESC.amt?smoney(ESC.share-ESC.amt):pts(G.seats[ms].pts)+' points');},1400);}}
  hud();
}

/* =====================================================================
   Starting games: people at a live table first (host first), regulars fill the rest
   ===================================================================== */
function botSeat(name){var r=regOf(name)||REG[0];return {id:'bot-'+r[0].toLowerCase(),name:r[0],kind:'bot',skill:opt.skill};}

/* =====================================================================
   Tutorial: one rigged practice hand with a coach. You build Posse (5555 5555 5555 FF):
   Charleston pass, call a Kong with a Joker, draw, throw, then call Mah Jongg on the last 5 Bam.
   ===================================================================== */
var TUT={on:false};
var CH_TUT=[{d:1,nm:'right'}];
var T_PASS=[0,68,108],T_GIFT=[54,137,24],T_KONG=90,T_LAST=55,T_DRAW=19,T_JUNK1=76,T_JUNK2=24;
function tutPosse(){for(var i=0;i<HANDS.length;i++)if(HANDS[i].name==='Posse')return i;return -1;}
function tutDeal(G){
  var P=[16,17,18,52,53,88,89,136,144,0,68,108,76],S3=[T_KONG,T_LAST].concat(T_GIFT),res={};
  P.concat(S3,[T_DRAW,91]).forEach(function(id){res[id]=1;});
  var rest=[];for(var i=0;i<152;i++)if(!res[i])rest.push(i);shuffle(rest,RNG);
  G.dealer=3;G.hand=1;
  G.seats[0].rack=P.slice();G.seats[1].rack=rest.splice(0,13);G.seats[2].rack=rest.splice(0,13);G.seats[3].rack=S3.concat(rest.splice(0,9));
  G.seats.forEach(function(sd){sd.exp=[];sd.dead=false;sd.tgt=-1;sd.calls=0;});
  rest.unshift(91);rest.splice(rest.length-3,0,T_DRAW);
  G.wall=rest;G.dis=[];G.last=null;G.turn=3;G.need='';G.drawn=-1;G.cl=null;G.elig=[];G.res=null;G.dl=0;G.cdl=0;
  G.phase='char';G.ch={i:0,st:CH_TUT,picks:[null,null,null,null],votes:[null,null,null,null]};
  pushEv(G,{kind:'deal',seat:-1});
}
function tutSafe(s,n){
  var sd=G.seats[s],out=[];
  sd.rack.forEach(function(id){var t=TT(id);if(out.length>=n||id===T_KONG||id===T_LAST||T_GIFT.indexOf(id)>=0||t===JK||t===FL||t===4||t===13||t===22)return;
    if(G.phase==='play'){for(var c=3;c<=5;c++)if(canExpose(G,0,t,c))return;if(canMjWith(G,0,id))return;}
    out.push(id);});
  return out;
}
function tutBot(s){
  var sd=G.seats[s];
  if(G.phase==='char')return {t:'pass',ids:s===3?T_GIFT.slice():tutSafe(s,3)};
  if(G.phase==='call')return {t:'nocall'};
  if(G.phase==='play'){
    if(s===3&&sd.rack.indexOf(T_KONG)>=0)return {t:'discard',id:T_KONG};
    var me=G.seats[0].rack;
    if(s===3&&sd.rack.indexOf(T_LAST)>=0&&me.indexOf(T_DRAW)>=0&&me.indexOf(T_JUNK2)<0)return {t:'discard',id:T_LAST};
    var c=tutSafe(s,1);return {t:'discard',id:c.length?c[0]:sd.rack.filter(function(id){return id!==T_LAST&&TT(id)!==JK;})[0]};
  }
  return null;
}
function botPlay(s){return TUT.on?tutBot(s):botAct(G,s,skillOf(s));}
function startTut(){
  cancelSteps();
  TUT={on:true,gid:'tut'+Date.now().toString(36),a:0,got:false,hold:false,glow:{},btn:'',expect:null,step:''};
  try{decideMode();}catch(e){}
  RNG=mulberry(20261030);
  var people=[{id:myId(),name:myName(),kind:'human'},botSeat('June'),botSeat('Walt'),botSeat('Rusty')];
  G=newGameState({seats:people,hands:1,copt:'skip',stake:0,gid:TUT.gid},RNG);
  tutDeal(G);TRK=tutPosse();ORDER=[];
  sheetUser='';hideSheet();shownTag='';
  publish();show();logLine('Tutorial: one practice hand','h');runStep();
}
function endTut(toSetup){
  TUT={on:false};var c=$('coach');if(c)c.remove();cancelSteps();
  G=null;TRK=-1;SEL=[];ORDER=[];rackSig='';actSig='';cardSig='';renderAll();
  try{scheduleDecide();}catch(e){}
  if(toSetup){sheetUser='';showSheet('setup');shownTag='setup:';}
}
var TUT_TXT={
  intro:['Welcome to Mahjong','This lesson plays one real hand with you. The goal: be the first to build one hand from <b>The Saddle Up Card</b> using exactly 14 tiles.'],
  card:['The card','The card lists every winning hand. We&rsquo;ll go for <b>Posse</b>: <b class="ta">5555</b> <b class="tb">5555</b> <b class="tc">5555</b> <b class="tf">FF</b>, four 5s in each suit plus 2 Flowers. It&rsquo;s lit up on the card%CARD%, and the green dots under your tiles mark the ones that fit.'],
  tiles:['Your tiles','Your rack holds Dots, Bams and Craks (1&ndash;9), winds, dragons, Flowers and a Joker. <b>Point at (or tap) any tile</b> to see what it is and where it counts on the card.'],
  pass:['The Charleston','First everyone passes 3 tiles they don&rsquo;t need. Pick the glowing <b>1 Dot, 9 Bam and North</b>, then press <b>Pass 3</b>. (Jokers can never be passed.)'],
  got:['Nice trade','Rusty passed you a <b>5 Bam</b>, a <b>Flower</b> and a 7 Dot. A real game passes right, across and left, and maybe a second round. Rusty is <b>East</b> (the dealer), so Rusty throws first.'],
  wait:['Watch the table','Each turn a player draws a tile from the wall and throws one. Thrown tiles land in the middle.'],
  call:['Call it!','Rusty threw a <b>5 Crak</b>! You have two 5 Craks and a Joker. Press <b>Kong</b> to call it: they go face up as four 5 Craks, with the Joker standing in for one.'],
  discard1:['Your throw','Called tiles sit face up (<b>exposed</b>) for everyone to see. Calling gives you the turn, so throw a tile: tap the glowing <b>2 Crak</b>, then press <b>Throw</b>.'],
  drew:['You drew a 5 Dot','That&rsquo;s four 5 Dots. Throw the glowing <b>7 Dot</b>. Now you only need one more <b>5 Bam</b>.'],
  mj:['Mah Jongg!','Rusty threw a <b>5 Bam</b>, your last tile! Press <b>Mah Jongg!</b> to win. (You may call any tile, even for a pair, when it wins.)']
};
function tutStep(){
  if(!TUT.on||!G||G.gid!==TUT.gid)return '';
  var me=G.seats[0];
  if(G.phase==='char'){if(TUT.a<3)return ['intro','card','tiles'][TUT.a];return G.ch.picks[0]?'wait':'pass';}
  if(G.phase==='call'){if(G.elig.indexOf(0)>=0&&!G.cl[0]){if(G.last.id===T_KONG)return 'call';if(G.last.id===T_LAST)return 'mj';}return 'wait';}
  if(G.phase==='play'){
    if(!TUT.got)return 'got';
    if(G.turn===0&&me.rack.indexOf(T_JUNK1)>=0&&me.exp.length)return 'discard1';
    if(G.turn===0&&me.rack.indexOf(T_DRAW)>=0&&me.rack.indexOf(T_JUNK2)>=0)return 'drew';
    return 'wait';}
  return '';
}
function tutTick(){
  if(!TUT.on)return;var st=tutStep(),glow={},btn='',ex=null;
  if(!TUT.trk&&G&&G.gid===TUT.gid){TUT.trk=true;TRK=tutPosse();cardSig='';rackSig='';renderCardSide(true);renderRack();}
  if(st==='pass'){T_PASS.forEach(function(id){glow[id]=1;});btn='pass';ex=function(a){return a.t==='pass'&&a.ids.length===3&&T_PASS.every(function(id){return a.ids.indexOf(id)>=0;});};}
  else if(st==='call'){btn='c4';ex=function(a){return a.t==='call'&&+a.c===4;};}
  else if(st==='discard1'){glow[T_JUNK1]=1;btn='discard';ex=function(a){return a.t==='discard'&&a.id===T_JUNK1;};}
  else if(st==='drew'){glow[T_JUNK2]=1;btn='discard';ex=function(a){return a.t==='discard'&&a.id===T_JUNK2;};}
  else if(st==='mj'){btn='cmj';ex=function(a){return a.t==='call'&&!!a.mj;};}
  TUT.hold=st==='got';
  var gk=Object.keys(glow).join(',');
  if(gk!==Object.keys(TUT.glow).join(',')||btn!==TUT.btn){TUT.glow=glow;TUT.btn=btn;rackSig='';actSig='';renderRack();renderActs();}
  TUT.expect=ex;
  if(st!==TUT.step){TUT.step=st;coach(st);}
}
function coach(st){
  var c=$('coach');
  if(!st||!TUT_TXT[st]){if(c)c.remove();return;}
  if(!c){c=document.createElement('div');c.id='coach';c.className='coach';stage.appendChild(c);
    c.addEventListener('click',function(e){var b=e.target.closest('button');if(!b)return;var a=b.getAttribute('data-c');
      if(a==='skip'){endTut(true);return;}
      if(a==='next'){if(TUT.step==='got'){TUT.got=true;TUT.hold=false;stepKey='';tutTick();runStep();return;}TUT.a++;tutTick();}});}
  var T=TUT_TXT[st],info=st==='intro'||st==='card'||st==='tiles'||st==='got',n=['intro','card','tiles','pass','got','call','discard1','drew','mj'].indexOf(st);
  var body=T[1].replace('%CARD%',felt.classList.contains('wide')?' to the right':' (press <b>Card</b> to see it)');
  c.className='coach'+(st==='wait'?' quiet':'');
  c.innerHTML='<div class="ctop"><span>Tutorial'+(n>=0?' &middot; '+(n+1)+' of 9':'')+'</span><button type="button" data-c="skip">Skip tutorial</button></div><b>'+T[0]+'</b><p>'+body+'</p>'+
    (info?'<div class="crow"><button type="button" class="btn gold" data-c="next">'+(st==='got'?'Start play':'Next')+' &rarr;</button></div>':'');
  c.classList.remove('pop');void c.offsetWidth;c.classList.add('pop');
  if(st==='card'||st==='intro'){var ci=$('cardIn'),tr=ci&&ci.querySelector('.hrow.trk');if(tr)ci.scrollTop=tr.offsetTop-90;}
}
function tutBlock(a){
  if(!TUT.on||!TUT.expect){toast(TUT.on?'Follow the tip first':'', TUT.on?'Press Next on the tutorial box':'',1600);return true;}
  if(TUT.expect(a))return false;
  var st=TUT.step;toast(st==='pass'?'Pass the 3 glowing tiles':st==='call'?'Press Kong for this one':st==='mj'?'Press Mah Jongg!':'Throw the glowing tile','This is the tutorial\u2019s plan',1800);
  SFX.bad();return true;
}
function tutDone(){
  var ms=mySeat(),r=G.res||{},won=r.w===ms;
  return '<div class="pill"><i></i><span>Tutorial complete</span></div><h2>'+(won?'You <span>Mah Jongg!</span>':'Hand <span>over</span>')+'</h2>'+
    '<div class="res"><em>Posse</em> &middot; '+(r.val||25)+' pts</div>'+
    '<div class="tutfin"><p><b>Scoring:</b> Rusty threw your winning tile, so Rusty pays double and everyone else pays once. Win on your own draw and <b>everyone</b> pays double. Win with <b>no jokers</b> and it doubles again.</p>'+
    '<ul><li><b>Suggest</b> picks good Charleston passes for you.</li><li>Green dots under your tiles show what fits your closest hand. Tap a hand on the card to <b>track</b> it instead.</li>'+
    '<li><b>Swap joker:</b> on your turn, trade the real tile for a joker in anyone&rsquo;s exposure.</li><li>If your exposed tiles can&rsquo;t make any hand on the card anymore, your hand goes <b>dead</b>.</li>'+
    '<li>Concealed hands (marked <b>C</b>) can&rsquo;t call tiles to expose, only to win.</li></ul></div>'+
    '<div class="row"><button type="button" class="btn gold go" data-act="tutreal">Play a real game &rarr;</button><button type="button" class="btn2" data-act="tutagain">Replay tutorial</button></div>';
}

function newGame(seed){
  if(TUT.on){TUT={on:false};var cz=$('coach');if(cz)cz.remove();TRK=-1;}
  cancelSteps();
  if(G&&G.seats&&ESC.amt&&!ESC.done&&ESC.gid===G.gid&&mySeat()>=0&&G.phase!=='over'){ESC.done=true;record.l++;}
  RNG=seed!==undefined&&seed!==null?mulberry(+seed):function(){return rnd(1e9)/1e9;};
  var people=[{id:myId(),name:myName(),kind:'human'}];
  if(LV.mode==='host')LV.roster.forEach(function(p){if(!p.isMe&&people.length<4)people.push({id:p.id,name:safeName(p.name),kind:'human'});});
  fixOpps();var names=OPP.slice(),used=people.map(function(x){return x.name;});
  while(people.length<4){var nm=names.shift();if(!nm||used.indexOf(nm)>=0){var pool=REG.filter(function(r){return used.indexOf(r[0])<0;});nm=pool[rnd(pool.length)][0];}used.push(nm);people.push(botSeat(nm));}
  G=newGameState({seats:people,hands:opt.hands,copt:opt.copt,stake:opt.stake,gid:'g'+Date.now().toString(36)+rnd(1e6).toString(36)},RNG);
  dealHand(G,RNG);G.dl=0;G.cdl=0;
  sheetUser='';hideSheet();
  publish();show();
  logLine('New game: '+G.hands+' hands &middot; '+(G.copt==='skip'?'one Charleston':'full Charleston')+' &middot; '+(G.stake?'stake '+money(G.stake):'for fun'),'h');
  runStep();return true;
}

/* =====================================================================
   Sheets: setup, rules, card, hand over, game over, live waiting
   ===================================================================== */
var STAKES=[0,10,25,100,500],CHIPC={10:'c5',25:'c25',100:'c100',500:'c500'};
var sheetKind='',sheetUser='',sheetT=0,shownTag='';
function stakeChips(){return STAKES.map(function(v){var dis=v&&v>bank;return '<button type="button" class="chip '+(v?CHIPC[v]:'fun')+(opt.stake===v?' on':'')+'" data-stake="'+v+'"'+(dis?' disabled':'')+'>'+(v?'$'+v:'Fun')+'</button>';}).join('');}
function segH(id,vals,cur){return '<div class="seg" data-sh="'+id+'">'+vals.map(function(v){return '<button type="button" data-v="'+v[0]+'" class="'+(String(cur)===String(v[0])?'on':'')+'">'+v[1]+'</button>';}).join('')+'</div>';}
var RULES='<div class="rules">'+
  '<h4>The tiles</h4><ul><li>152 tiles: Dots, Bams and Craks numbered 1&ndash;9 (four of each), four of each wind, four of each dragon, 8 flowers and 8 jokers.</li>'+
  '<li>Each suit has its dragon: Dots go with Soap, Bams with Green, Craks with Red.</li></ul>'+
  '<div class="tiles">'+typeH(4)+typeH(13)+typeH(22)+typeH(27)+typeH(28)+typeH(31)+typeH(32)+typeH(33)+typeH(34)+typeH(35)+'</div>'+
  '<h4>The goal</h4><ul><li>Be first to make one of the 40 hands on <b>The Saddle Up Card</b> with 14 tiles. Every hand is exact: the right groups, nothing extra.</li>'+
  '<li>Groups: a pair (2), pung (3), kong (4) and quint (5). Jokers stand in for any tile in a group of 3 or more, never in a pair or a single.</li></ul>'+
  '<h4>The Charleston</h4><ul><li>East (the dealer) starts with 14 tiles, everyone else 13.</li><li>Pass 3 tiles right, then across, then left. Then the table may agree to a second Charleston (left, across, right). Anyone can stop it.</li>'+
  '<li>Last comes an optional courtesy pass across: 0 to 3 tiles, you swap the smaller number. Jokers are never passed.</li></ul>'+
  '<h4>Play</h4><ul><li>East throws first. Then each player draws from the wall and throws one tile, to the right around the table.</li>'+
  '<li>When a tile is thrown you may <b>call</b> it to complete a pung, kong or quint for a hand you are making: those tiles go face up (exposed) and you throw next. Pairs can only be called to win.</li>'+
  '<li>Mah Jongg beats a call for an exposure; otherwise the player next in turn gets it. Concealed (C) hands can&rsquo;t expose, but may still win on a thrown tile.</li>'+
  '<li><b>Joker swap:</b> on your turn, trade the real tile for a joker in any exposure and keep the joker.</li>'+
  '<li>If your exposures can no longer make any hand on the card, your hand is <b>dead</b>: you sit out the rest of that hand and still pay.</li></ul>'+
  '<h4>Paying</h4><ul><li>Win off a throw: the thrower pays double the hand&rsquo;s value, the others pay it once. Win on your own draw: everyone pays double.</li>'+
  '<li>No jokers in the winning hand (jokerless) doubles it again. A wall game pays nobody.</li>'+
  '<li>With a stake, everyone antes into the pot and the final points decide how the pot is split.</li></ul>'+
  '<h4>Controls</h4><ul><li>Tap a tile to pick it, tap again (or press Throw) to throw it. Drag tiles to rearrange your rack, or press Sort.</li><li>Calls pop up for a few seconds after each throw when you can use the tile. <kbd>Space</kbd> does the main action.</li></ul></div>';
function showSheet(kind){
  sheetKind=kind;var h='',ms=mySeat(),sh=$('sheet');sh.className='sheet'+(kind==='card'||kind==='rules'?' wide':'');
  if(kind==='setup'){
    if(opt.stake>bank)opt.stake=0;fixOpps();
    h='<div class="pill"><i></i><span>'+(LV.mode==='host'?'Live table '+(LV.L?LV.L.table:''):'Game Parlor')+'</span></div><h2>Pull up a chair for <span>mahjong</span></h2>'+
      '<div class="opps">'+OPP.map(function(nm){var r=regOf(nm);return '<div class="opp"><span class="av" style="background:'+r[1]+'"><span>'+nm.charAt(0)+'</span></span><span class="nm">'+nm+'</span><small>'+r[2]+'</small></div>';}).join('')+'</div>'+
      '<div class="row" style="margin:0 0 4px"><button type="button" class="btn2" data-act="reshuffle" style="min-height:40px;padding:6px 12px;font-size:12.5px">New faces</button></div>'+
      '<div class="srow"><span>Difficulty</span>'+segH('skill',[['easy','Easy'],['normal','Normal'],['hard','Hard']],opt.skill)+'</div>'+
      '<div class="srow"><span>Charleston</span>'+segH('copt',[['std','Standard'],['skip','Skip 2nd']],opt.copt)+'</div>'+
      '<div class="srow"><span>Hands</span>'+segH('hands',[[4,'4'],[8,'8'],[16,'16']],opt.hands)+'</div>'+
      '<div class="srow"><span>Stake</span><div class="stakes">'+stakeChips()+'</div></div>'+
      '<p class="note">Points settle after every hand. With a stake, everyone antes in and the final points split the pot.'+(LV.mode==='host'?' People at the table sit in; regulars fill the empty seats.':'')+'</p>'+
      '<div class="row"><button type="button" class="btn gold go" data-act="start">Shuffle &amp; deal &rarr;</button></div>'+
      (LV.mode!=='guest'?'<div class="row"><button type="button" class="btn2 tutgo" data-act="tutstart">New to mahjong? <b>Play the tutorial</b></button></div>':'')+
      '<div class="row"><button type="button" class="btn2" data-act="rules">How to play</button><button type="button" class="btn2" data-act="card">The Saddle Up Card</button>'+(sheetUser==='setup'&&G&&G.seats&&G.phase!=='over'?'<button type="button" class="btn2" data-act="close">Back to the game</button>':'')+'</div>';
  } else if(kind==='rules'){
    h='<h2>How to <span>play</span></h2>'+RULES+'<div class="row"><button type="button" class="btn gold" data-act="close">Got it</button>'+(LV.mode!=='guest'?'<button type="button" class="btn2" data-act="tutstart">Play the tutorial</button>':'')+'</div>';
  } else if(kind==='card'){
    h='<div class="cardbox">'+cardHTML()+'</div><div class="row"><button type="button" class="btn gold" data-act="close">Back to the table</button></div>';
  } else if(kind==='over'&&TUT.on){
    h=tutDone();
  } else if(kind==='round'||kind==='over'){
    var r=G.res||{},w=r.w,hd=w>=0?HANDS[r.h]:null;
    var tiles='';if(w>=0){var sd=G.seats[w];tiles='<div class="whand">'+sd.exp.map(function(e){return '<span class="eg">'+e.ids.map(function(id){return tileH(id);}).join('')+'</span>';}).join('')+'<span class="eg">'+sortIds(sd.rack).map(function(id){return tileH(id);}).join('')+'</span></div>';}
    h='<div class="pill"><i></i><span>Hand '+r.hand+' of '+G.hands+'</span></div>'+
      '<h2>'+(w>=0?(w===ms?'You <span>Mah Jongg!</span>':esc(nameOf(w))+' <span>Mah Jongg!</span>'):'Wall <span>game</span>')+'</h2>'+
      (hd?'<div class="res"><em>'+esc(hd.name)+'</em> &middot; '+CATS.filter(function(c){return c.k===hd.cat;})[0].n+' &middot; '+r.val+' pts'+(r.jl?' (jokerless &times;2)':'')+'</div><div class="res" style="font-size:13px;color:#94a3b8">'+(r.how==='self'?'Self-drawn: everyone pays double':esc(nameOf(r.by))+' threw it and pays double')+'</div>'+tiles:'<p>The wall ran out before anybody made a hand. Nobody pays.</p>')+
      scoreTable(true);
    if(kind==='over'){var iw=G.win===ms,shs=shares(G);
      h+='<div class="res" style="margin-top:6px">'+(iw?'You win the game':esc(nameOf(G.win))+' wins the game')+' with <em>'+pts(G.seats[G.win].pts)+'</em></div>'+
        '<p>'+(ESC.amt&&ms>=0?'Pot '+money(ESC.amt*4)+': your share <b>'+money(ESC.share||0)+'</b> ('+smoney((ESC.share||0)-ESC.amt)+'). Bankroll <b>'+money(bank)+'</b>.':(shs?'Pot '+money(G.stake*4)+' split by points.':'Played for fun.'))+' Record '+record.w+'&ndash;'+record.l+'.</p>'+
        (LV.mode==='guest'?'<p>Waiting for the host to deal again&hellip;</p>':'<div class="row"><button type="button" class="btn gold go" data-act="again">'+(LV.mode==='host'?'Deal again':'Play again')+' &rarr;</button><button type="button" class="btn2" data-act="change">Change table</button></div>');
    } else h+=(LV.mode==='guest'?'<p>Next hand coming up&hellip;</p>':'<div class="row"><button type="button" class="btn gold go" data-act="next">Next hand &rarr;</button></div>');
  } else if(kind==='gwait'){
    h='<div class="pill"><i></i><span>Live table '+(LV.L?LV.L.table:'')+'</span></div><h2>Sitting down with <span>'+esc(LV.hostName||'the host')+'</span></h2><p>'+esc(LV.hostName||'The host')+' deals the next game. Regulars fill any empty seats.</p>';
  }
  if(kind==='card'||kind==='rules')h='<button type="button" class="shx" data-act="close" aria-label="Close"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.8" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18"/></svg></button>'+h;
  sh.innerHTML=h;$('ov').classList.remove('hide');$('ov').scrollTop=0;renderActs();
}
function hideSheet(){$('ov').classList.add('hide');sheetKind='';}
function syncSheet(){
  if(sheetUser)return;
  var want='';
  if(!G||!G.seats)want=LV.mode==='guest'?'gwait':'setup';
  else if(G.phase==='round')want='round';else if(G.phase==='over')want='over';
  var tag=want+':'+(G?G.gid+':'+G.hand:'');
  if(!want){clearTimeout(sheetT);if(sheetKind)hideSheet();shownTag='';return;}
  if(shownTag===tag)return;
  shownTag=tag;
  if(want==='round'||want==='over'){clearTimeout(sheetT);sheetT=later(D(1600),function(){if(!sheetUser&&G&&(G.phase+':'+G.gid+':'+G.hand)===tag)showSheet(G.phase);});return;}
  showSheet(want);
}
function openCard(){if(felt.classList.contains('wide')){var ci=$('cardIn');renderCardSide(true);var t=ci.querySelector('.hrow.trk,.hrow.close');if(t)ci.scrollTop=t.offsetTop-80;return;}sheetUser='card';showSheet('card');}
$('sheet').addEventListener('click',function(e){
  if(sheetKind==='card'&&e.target.closest('[data-trk]')){onTrack(e);return;}
  var b=e.target.closest('button');if(!b||b.disabled)return;
  var sh=b.parentNode&&b.parentNode.getAttribute&&b.parentNode.getAttribute('data-sh');
  if(sh){var v=b.getAttribute('data-v');clickSeg({skill:'optSkill',copt:'optChar',hands:'optHands'}[sh],v);showSheet('setup');return;}
  if(b.hasAttribute('data-stake')){opt.stake=+b.getAttribute('data-stake');[].forEach.call($('sheet').querySelectorAll('[data-stake]'),function(x){x.classList.toggle('on',x===b);});SFX.clack();hud();return;}
  var a=b.getAttribute('data-act');
  if(a==='tutstart'||a==='tutagain'){startTut();return;}
  if(a==='tutreal'){endTut(true);return;}
  if(a==='start'||a==='again'){if(LV.mode==='guest')return;newGame();}
  else if(a==='reshuffle'){OPP=pickOpps(3);seatSig={};renderSeats();showSheet('setup');}
  else if(a==='rules'){sheetUser='rules';showSheet('rules');}
  else if(a==='card'){sheetUser='card';showSheet('card');}
  else if(a==='change'){sheetUser='setup';showSheet('setup');}
  else if(a==='next'){if(ENG)nextHand();}
  else if(a==='close'){var back=sheetKind==='card'||sheetKind==='rules';sheetUser='';hideSheet();shownTag='';if(back&&(!G||!G.seats)){showSheet('setup');shownTag='setup:';}else syncSheet();}
});

/* =====================================================================
   Input: rack taps, drags, buttons, keys
   ===================================================================== */
function shake(id){var b=$('rin').querySelector('[data-id="'+id+'"]');if(!b)return;b.classList.remove('shake');void b.offsetWidth;b.classList.add('shake');}
function tapTile(id){
  if(!G||!G.seats)return;var ms=mySeat();if(ms<0)return;
  if(G.phase==='char'&&myPending()){
    var st=chStage(G);if(st.v)return;
    if(TT(id)===JK){shake(id);SFX.bad();toast('Jokers can\u2019t be passed','',1100);return;}
    var i=SEL.indexOf(id);if(i>=0)SEL.splice(i,1);else{if(SEL.length>=3)SEL.shift();SEL.push(id);}
    renderRack();renderActs();status();return;}
  if(myTurn()){
    if(SEL.length===1&&SEL[0]===id){myAct({t:'discard',id:id});return;}
    SEL=[id];renderRack();renderActs();status();return;}
  SEL=SEL[0]===id?[]:[id];renderRack();
}
var drag=null,justDragged=0;
$('rin').addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){var b=e.target.closest('.rkt');if(!b||e.button>0)return;drag={id:+b.getAttribute('data-id'),x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,el:b,on:false};});
addEventListener('pointermove',function(e){
  if(!drag)return;
  if(!drag.on){if(Math.abs(e.clientX-drag.x)+Math.abs(e.clientY-drag.y)<10)return;drag.on=true;
    var g=drag.el.cloneNode(true);g.classList.remove('sel','dealt','shake');g.classList.add('ghost');g.style.fontSize=getComputedStyle($('rin')).fontSize;document.body.appendChild(g);drag.g=g;drag.el.classList.add('drag');}
  var r=drag.el.getBoundingClientRect();drag.g.style.left=(e.clientX-r.width/2)+'px';drag.g.style.top=(e.clientY-r.height/2)+'px';
  /* where it would land: the nearest tile center */
  var best=-1,bd=1e9,after=false;[].forEach.call($('rin').children,function(c,i){var q=c.getBoundingClientRect(),cx=q.left+q.width/2,cy=q.top+q.height/2,d=Math.abs(e.clientX-cx)+Math.abs(e.clientY-cy)*1.5;if(d<bd){bd=d;best=i;after=e.clientX>cx;}});
  drag.to=best;drag.after=after;
});
addEventListener('pointerup',function(e){
  if(!drag)return;var d=drag;drag=null;
  if(!d.on){if(e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.rkt')===d.el)tapTile(d.id);return;}
  if(d.g)d.g.remove();d.el.classList.remove('drag');justDragged=Date.now();
  if(d.to>=0){var tid=+$('rin').children[d.to].getAttribute('data-id');if(tid!==d.id){var o=ORDER.filter(function(x){return x!==d.id;}),j=o.indexOf(tid);o.splice(j+(d.after?1:0),0,d.id);ORDER=o;TRKMAN=true;GAP={};EXTRA={};}}
  rackSig='';renderRack();
});
addEventListener('pointercancel',function(){if(drag){if(drag.g)drag.g.remove();drag.el.classList.remove('drag');drag=null;}});
/* keyboard users: Enter/Space on a focused tile */
$('rin').addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.detail!==0)return;var b=e.target.closest('.rkt');if(b)tapTile(+b.getAttribute('data-id'));});
/* tap a glowing joker in an exposure to swap for it */
function onExpTap(e){var t=e.target.closest('.tile.sw');if(!t||!myTurn())return;var eg=t.closest('.eg'),p=+eg.getAttribute('data-s'),ei=+eg.getAttribute('data-e'),o=swapOpts(mySeat()).filter(function(x){return x.p===p&&x.e===ei;})[0];if(o)myAct({t:'swap',id:o.id,p:o.p,e:o.e});}
stage.addEventListener('click',onExpTap);$('mexp').addEventListener('click',onExpTap);
$('acts').addEventListener('click',function(e){var b=e.target.closest('button');if(!b||b.disabled)return;doButton(b.getAttribute('data-a'));});
function doButton(a){
  var ms=mySeat();
  if(a==='deal'){if(sheetKind==='setup')newGame();else{sheetUser='';showSheet('setup');}return;}
  if(a==='next'){if(ENG)nextHand();return;}
  if(a==='again'){if(ENG)newGame();return;}
  if(!G||ms<0)return;
  if(a==='vote0'||a==='vote1'){myAct({t:'vote',go:a==='vote1'});return;}
  if(a==='suggest'&&TUT.on){SEL=T_PASS.slice();renderRack();renderActs();status();return;}
  if(a==='suggest'){var st=chStage(G),c=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(G)),act=botAct(c,ms,'hard');SEL=act&&act.ids?act.ids.slice(0,st.c?3:3):[];renderRack();renderActs();status();return;}
  if(a==='pass'){myAct({t:'pass',ids:SEL.slice()});SEL=[];return;}
  if(a==='discard'){if(SEL.length===1)myAct({t:'discard',id:SEL[0]});return;}
  if(a==='mj'){myAct({t:'mj'});return;}
  if(a==='swap'){var o=swapOpts(ms),pick1=o.filter(function(x){return SEL.indexOf(x.id)>=0;})[0]||o[0];if(pick1)myAct({t:'swap',id:pick1.id,p:pick1.p,e:pick1.e});return;}
  if(a==='cmj'){myAct({t:'call',mj:1});return;}
  if(a==='c3'||a==='c4'||a==='c5'){myAct({t:'call',c:+a.slice(1)});return;}
  if(a==='nocall'){myAct({t:'nocall'});return;}
}
function mainAct(){var bs=$('acts').querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled])'),k=null;[].forEach.call(bs,function(b){if(b.querySelector('kbd'))k=b;});if(k)doButton(k.getAttribute('data-a'));}
$('sortBtn').addEventListener('click',function(){var ms=mySeat();if(!G||ms<0)return;if(TRK>=0&&TRKMAN){TRKMAN=false;trackSort(true);}else{ORDER=sortIds(G.seats[ms].rack);GAP={};EXTRA={};TRKMAN=true;}rackSig='';renderRack();});
$('cardBtn').addEventListener('click',openCard);$('cardBtn2').addEventListener('click',function(){sheetUser='card';showSheet('card');});
document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){
  if(e.altKey||e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)return;if(e.target&&(e.target.tagName==='INPUT'||e.target.tagName==='TEXTAREA'))return;
  var k=e.key.toLowerCase();
  if(k===' '||k==='enter'){if(e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.rkt'))return;if(!$('ov').classList.contains('hide')&&sheetKind!=='round'&&sheetKind!=='over')return;e.preventDefault();mainAct();}
  else if(k==='m'){var b=$('acts').querySelector('.mjb');if(b)b.click();}
  else if(k==='s'){$('sortBtn').click();}
  else if(k==='c'){openCard();}
  else if(k==='escape'){SEL=[];renderRack();renderActs();}
});
$('newBtn').addEventListener('click',function(){if(LV.mode==='guest')return;sheetUser='setup';showSheet('setup');});
$('rulesBtn').addEventListener('click',function(){sheetUser='rules';showSheet('rules');});

/* ---------- settings ---------- */
function seg(id,fn){[].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#'+id+' button'),function(b){b.addEventListener('click',function(){if(b.disabled)return;
  [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#'+id+' button'),function(x){x.classList.toggle('on',x===b);});fn(b.getAttribute('data-v'));});});}
function clickSeg(id,v){var b=document.querySelector('#'+id+' button[data-v="'+v+'"]');if(b&&!b.disabled)b.click();}
seg('optSkill',function(v){opt.skill=v;if(G&&G.seats&&LV.mode!=='guest')G.seats.forEach(function(sd){if(sd.kind==='bot')sd.skill=v;});});
seg('optChar',function(v){opt.copt=v==='skip'?'skip':'std';});
seg('optHands',function(v){opt.hands=+v||8;hud();});
seg('optHints',function(v){opt.hints=v==='1';rackSig='';renderRack();renderMine();});
seg('optSpeed',function(v){opt.speed=+v||1;});
seg('optTalk',function(v){opt.talk=v==='1';});
seg('optSound',function(v){opt.sound=v==='1';});
seg('optLive',function(v){var on=v==='1';if(on===LV.enabled)return;LV.enabled=on;
  if(LV.L&&LV.L.available()&&booted){if(on){LV.settled=false;LV.L.start();setTimeout(function(){if(!LV.settled){LV.settled=true;decideMode();}},3500);}else{LV.L.stop();LV.settled=true;decideMode();}}
  renderLive();});

/* =====================================================================
   Live table: four seats. The host (longest seated) shuffles, deals, runs the regulars and settles every call;
   everyone else sends their moves. If the host leaves, the next person picks the hand up where it was.
   ===================================================================== */
var LV={L:null,chat:null,enabled:true,mode:'solo',settled:false,roster:[],hostId:null,hostName:'',hb:0,decT:0,sent:false,sentT:0,lastSt:null,stAt:0,dlLocal:0,cdlLocal:0,goneAt:{},toldBusy:false,helloT:0};
function renderLive(){
  var L=LV.L,av=!!(L&&L.available()),st=!av?'na':!LV.enabled?'off':L.status;
  var lbl={na:'Not available here',off:'Offline: you play the regulars',connecting:'Connecting\u2026',live:'Live',error:'Could not connect. Playing the regulars.'}[st]||'Offline';
  $('ldot').className='ldot '+(st==='live'?'live':st==='connecting'?'connecting':'');
  $('lstat').textContent=lbl;$('ltable').textContent=st==='live'?'Table '+L.table+(LV.mode==='host'?' \u00b7 you deal':''):'';
  var r=st==='live'?LV.roster:[];
  $('lroster').innerHTML=r.length?r.map(function(x){return '<span'+(x.isMe?' class="me"':'')+'>'+esc(safeName(x.name))+(x.isHost?' &middot; host':'')+(x.isMe?' (you)':'')+'</span>';}).join('')+(r.length<2?'<i>Waiting for people. Anyone who opens Mahjong now sits at your table.</i>':''):'<i>'+(st==='live'?'':'Turn this on to play other people on the site when they&rsquo;re here.')+'</i>';
  var ni=$('lname');if(L&&document.activeElement!==ni)ni.value=L.me.name;ni.disabled=!av;
  $('optLive').classList.toggle('dis',!av);
  var g=LV.mode==='guest';['rowSkill','rowChar','rowHands'].forEach(function(id){$(id).classList.toggle('locked',g);});
  $('newBtn').disabled=g;
  var lc=$('livechip');lc.classList.toggle('hide',LV.mode==='solo');lc.textContent='LIVE \u00b7 '+LV.roster.length;
}
function scheduleDecide(){clearTimeout(LV.decT);LV.decT=setTimeout(decideMode,150);}
function decideMode(){
  var L=LV.L,want='solo';
  if(L&&LV.enabled&&L.available()&&!AWAY&&L.status==='live'&&LV.roster.length>=2){if(!LV.settled)return;want=L.isHost()?'host':'guest';}
  if(TUT.on)want='solo';
  if(want===LV.mode){if(want==='host')fixPeople();renderLive();return;}
  var was=LV.mode;
  if(want==='guest'&&was==='solo'&&G&&G.seats&&!idle()){
    if(!LV.toldBusy){LV.toldBusy=true;toast((LV.hostName||'Someone')+' is dealing a live table','Finish this game to sit down',2600);}
    clearTimeout(LV.decT);LV.decT=setTimeout(decideMode,1500);renderLive();return;}
  LV.toldBusy=false;LV.mode=want;LV.sent=false;selfKey='';stepKey='';
  clearInterval(LV.hb);clearInterval(LV.helloT);
  if(want==='guest'){
    ENG=false;cancelSteps();if(was==='solo'){G=null;lastEvN=-1;lastGid='';}
    var hi=function(){try{LV.L.send('hello',{name:LV.L.me.name},LV.hostId);}catch(e){}};hi();
    LV.helloT=setInterval(function(){if(LV.mode!=='guest'){clearInterval(LV.helloT);return;}if(!LV.lastSt||Date.now()-LV.stAt>7000)hi();},2000);
    shownTag='';sheetUser='';renderAll();status();syncSheet();
    logLine('Live table '+L.table+': '+esc(LV.hostName)+' deals','h');
  } else {
    var adopt=!ENG;ENG=true;
    if(adopt&&G&&G.seats){G.dl=0;if(G.phase==='call')G.cdl=Date.now()+CALL_MS;}
    if(want==='host'){LV.hb=setInterval(function(){if(LV.mode==='host'){fixPeople();publish();}},2000);}
    fixPeople();
    if(adopt)logLine(want==='host'?'You\u2019re the host now':'Back to the regulars','h');
    stepKey='';shownTag='';if(!G||!G.seats){renderAll();syncSheet();}else{publish();show();}
    runStep();
  }
  renderLive();renderActs();
  try{if(LV.chat)LV.chat.refresh();}catch(e){}
}
/* people who left hand their seat to a regular after a short grace; coming back takes it back */
function fixPeople(){
  if(!ENG||!G||!G.seats)return;
  var here={},now=Date.now(),ch=false;LV.roster.forEach(function(p){here[p.id]=p;});here[myId()]={id:myId(),name:myName()};
  G.seats.forEach(function(sd){
    if(sd.kind==='human'&&!here[sd.id]){
      LV.goneAt[sd.id]=LV.goneAt[sd.id]||now;
      if(LV.mode==='solo'||now-LV.goneAt[sd.id]>8000){var r=REG.filter(function(x){return !G.seats.some(function(y){return y.name===x[0];});})[0]||REG[0];
        sd.was=sd.id;sd.wasName=sd.name;sd.kind='bot';sd.skill='normal';sd.name=r[0];sd.id='bot-'+r[0].toLowerCase();ch=true;logLine(esc(sd.wasName)+' left. '+r[0]+' takes the seat','l');}}
    else if(sd.kind==='human'){delete LV.goneAt[sd.id];}
    else if(sd.was&&here[sd.was]&&LV.mode==='host'){sd.kind='human';sd.id=sd.was;sd.name=sd.wasName||safeName(here[sd.was].name);delete sd.was;delete sd.wasName;ch=true;logLine(esc(sd.name)+' is back','h');}
  });
  if(ch){stepKey='';seatSig={};publish();show();runStep();}
}
function publish(to){if(LV.mode!=='host'||!LV.L||!G)return;try{var now=Date.now();LV.L.send('st',{g:G,tl:G.dl?Math.max(0,G.dl-now):0,cl:G.cdl?Math.max(0,G.cdl-now):0},to);}catch(e){}}
function onState(d,from){
  if(LV.mode!=='guest'||!d||!d.g||from!==LV.hostId)return;
  var g=d.g;if(!g.seats||!Array.isArray(g.seats)||g.seats.length!==4)return;
  LV.lastSt=d;LV.stAt=Date.now();
  LV.dlLocal=d.tl?Date.now()+d.tl:0;LV.cdlLocal=d.cl?Date.now()+d.cl:0;
  var same=G&&G.gid===g.gid&&G.evn===g.evn;
  if(same){var a=JSON.stringify(G.seats.map(function(x){return [x.id,x.kind,x.name];})),b=JSON.stringify(g.seats.map(function(x){return [x.id,x.kind,x.name];}));if(a!==b){G=g;seatSig={};renderAll();}return;}
  LV.sent=false;clearTimeout(LV.sentT);
  G=g;show();
}
function onAct(d,from){
  if(LV.mode!=='host'||!G||!d||!d.a||d.gid!==G.gid)return;
  var s=-1;for(var i=0;i<4;i++)if(G.seats[i].kind==='human'&&G.seats[i].id===from)s=i;
  if(s<0||pending(G).indexOf(s)<0){publish(from);return;}
  if(G.evn!==d.n){publish(from);return;}
  var a=d.a,c={t:String(a.t||'')};
  if(c.t==='pass')c.ids=(Array.isArray(a.ids)?a.ids:[]).map(Number).slice(0,3);
  else if(c.t==='vote')c.go=!!a.go;
  else if(c.t==='discard')c.id=+a.id;
  else if(c.t==='swap'){c.id=+a.id;c.p=+a.p;c.e=+a.e;}
  else if(c.t==='call'){if(a.mj)c.mj=1;else c.c=+a.c;}
  else if(c.t!=='mj'&&c.t!=='nocall'){publish(from);return;}
  if(!doAct(s,c))publish(from);
}
/* clocks: the call window, live turn timers; the host plays for anyone who runs out of time */
setInterval(function(){
  if(!G||!G.seats)return;var now=Date.now();
  if(ENG){
    if(G.phase==='call'&&G.cdl&&now>G.cdl){G.cdl=0;doAct(-1,{t:'close'});return;}
    if(LV.mode!=='solo'&&G.dl&&now>G.dl&&(G.phase==='char'||G.phase==='play')){G.dl=0;
      var acts=pending(G).filter(function(s){return G.seats[s].kind==='human';}).map(function(s){logLine(esc(nameOf(s))+' ran out of time &ndash; auto play','');return [s,botAct(G,s,'normal')];});
      if(acts.length)doActs(acts);return;}
  }
  /* call countdown bar */
  if(calloutOn){var cdl=ENG?G.cdl:LV.cdlLocal,tot=LV.mode==='solo'?CALL_MS:CALL_MS+700,f=cdl?clamp((cdl-now)/tot,0,1):1;var bi=$('callout').querySelector('.cbar i');if(bi)bi.style.transform='scaleX('+f.toFixed(3)+')';}
  /* turn clocks on the plates (live) */
  [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.tmr.on'),function(x){x.classList.remove('on');});
  if(LV.mode!=='solo'&&(G.phase==='char'||G.phase==='play')){var dl=ENG?G.dl:LV.dlLocal;if(dl){var left=Math.max(0,Math.ceil((dl-now)/1000));
    pending(G).forEach(function(s){if(G.seats[s].kind!=='human')return;var el=seatEl(s),t=el&&el.querySelector('.tmr');if(t){t.textContent=left+'s';t.classList.add('on');t.classList.toggle('low',left<=8);}});}}
},250);
function initLive(){
  if(typeof Live==='undefined')return;
  var L;try{L=Live.create({game:'mahjong',maxSeats:4});}catch(e){return;}
  LV.L=L;
  try{LV.chat=Live.chat(L,felt,{local:function(){return LV.mode==='solo'&&!!G&&!!G.seats;},localLabel:'The regulars',myName:function(){return LV.mode==='solo'?'You':L.me.name;},onMessage:onTableChat});}catch(e){}
  setInterval(function(){try{if(LV.chat)LV.chat.refresh();}catch(e){}},1500);
  L.on('status',function(s){
    if(s==='live'&&!LV.settled)setTimeout(function(){LV.settled=true;decideMode();},900);
    else if(s==='off'||s==='error'){LV.settled=true;scheduleDecide();}
    else scheduleDecide();
    renderLive();});
  L.on('host',scheduleDecide);
  L.on('roster',function(list){
    LV.roster=list||[];var h=null;LV.roster.forEach(function(x){if(x.isHost)h=x;});LV.hostId=h?h.id:null;LV.hostName=h?safeName(h.name):'';
    renderLive();scheduleDecide();});
  L.on('msg:hello',function(d,from){if(LV.mode==='host')publish(from);});
  L.on('msg:act',onAct);
  L.on('msg:st',onState);
  L.on('msg:chat',function(m,from){if(!m||!m.text||!G||!G.seats)return;var nm=String(m.name||'');for(var i=0;i<4;i++){var sd=G.seats[i];if(safeName(sd.name)===safeName(nm)&&(m.bot?sd.kind==='bot':sd.id===from)){bubble(i,String(m.text).slice(0,120));break;}}});
  $('lname').addEventListener('change',function(){var v=safeName(this.value);this.value=v;L.setName(v);if(ENG&&G&&G.seats){var s=mySeat();if(s>=0){G.seats[s].name=v;publish();}}renderLive();renderMine();});
  renderLive();
}
function onTableChat(m){
  if(!opt.talk||LV.mode!=='solo'||!G||!G.seats||typeof Live==='undefined'||!Live.banter)return;
  var bots=G.seats.filter(function(sd){return sd.kind==='bot';}).map(function(sd){var r=regOf(sd.name)||REG[0];return {id:'bot:'+r[0].toLowerCase(),name:r[0],key:r[3],col:r[1]};});
  Live.banter(m.text,bots,{game:'mahjong',
    advice:function(){var ms=mySeat(),n=ms>=0?nearHands(ms):[];return n.length?'You look closest to '+HANDS[n[0].h].name+', partner. Keep those jokers.':pick(['Never throw a joker you can use.','Watch what folks expose.']);},
    mood:function(b){var sd=G.seats.filter(function(x){return x.name===b.name;})[0];if(!sd)return 'even';return sd.pts>0?'up':sd.pts<0?'down':'even';},
    say:function(bb,txt,delay){setTimeout(function(){for(var i=0;i<4;i++)if(G.seats[i].name===bb.name){bubble(i,txt);break;}try{LV.chat.post(bb.id,bb.name,txt,bb.col);}catch(e){}},delay||900);}});
}

function findTile(t,avoid){
  var spots=[['wall',G.wall]];G.seats.forEach(function(sd,i){if(i!==avoid)spots.push(['r'+i,sd.rack]);});
  for(var k=0;k<spots.length;k++){var a=spots[k][1];for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++)if(TT(a[i])===t)return {arr:a,i:i};}return null;
}

function boot(){if(booted)return;booted=true;
  if(LV.L&&LV.L.available()&&LV.enabled){LV.L.start();setTimeout(function(){if(!LV.settled){LV.settled=true;decideMode();}},3500);}else{LV.settled=true;decideMode();}}

})();

<script>
(function(){
  var last=0;
  var sent=document.createElement('div');
  sent.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  sent.style.cssText='height:0;margin:0;padding:0;border:0;clear:both';
  function measure(){
    if(!document.body) return 0;
    if(sent.parentNode!==document.body) document.body.appendChild(sent);
    var pb=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.body).paddingBottom)||0;
    return Math.ceil(sent.getBoundingClientRect().bottom+(window.pageYOffset||0)+pb);
  }
  function post(){
    var h=measure();
    if(!h||Math.abs(h-last)<2) return;
    last=h;
    try{parent.postMessage({arcade:'h',h:h},'*');}catch(e){}
  }
  addEventListener('load',post); addEventListener('resize',post);
  if(window.ResizeObserver){
    try{ var ro=new ResizeObserver(post); ro.observe(document.body);
         var w=document.querySelector('.wrap'); if(w) ro.observe(w); }catch(e){}
  }
  document.addEventListener('load',post,true);
  addEventListener('message',function(e){if(e.data&&e.data.arcade==='visible'&&e.data.on){last=0;setTimeout(post,30);}});
  setInterval(post,900); post();
})();
